## Vāsudeva ḍasa

*My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

In this glorious day of your appearance I offer my humble obeisances at your lotus feet again and again.

When we were burning in the fire of material existence you came to deliver us. You are so causelessly merciful that you have given us secure shelter at your lotus feet.

Compared to my other GBC Godbrothers, my service to you is like the service the spider tried to offer to Lord Rāmacandra in building the bridge to Laṅkā. I am like that spider, sincerely trying to serve you the best I can.

Please shower your causeless mercy upon us so that we will be able to fulfill your desire of making Fiji the first Kṛṣṇa conscious country. Please let me remain in your service eternally.

Your eternal servant,  
Vāsudeva dāsa

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from the ISKCON Centers

## Accra, Ghana

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Have Thine own way, Lord, have Thine own way.  
Thou art the potter, we are the clay.  
Mold us and make us in Thine own way;  
Have Thine own way, Lord, have Thine own way.

*Dear Jagad-guru,*

You STATED: Until one becomes free from sinful reactions, one cannot glorify the Lord. Because of our sinful disposition, we are always in an awkward position. We are always at a loss for words in the matter of glorifying Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet, as you are a father with the highest quality of an ocean of I mercy. Let there be singing and words of praise to our *ācārya,* Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Fulfillment within gazey, let there be silence and meditation on your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Upon your words, contemplation within our hearts, a Śrīla Prabhupāda, let there dwell. Your desires fulfilled as one's own to compel. Let it with gravity be understood that as *ācārya*, and in the human presence, His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda has appeared for our deliverance. Following in the footsteps of Lord Caitanya, you showed us what a devotee of Kṛṣṇa is like. The purity of your preaching has pierced our hearts due to the power of your personal example as *ācārya*. You are our eternal grandfather and well-wisher, and we want to thank you with all the gratitude we can muster for letting us live in the association of the great Vaiṣṇavas.

You have let us catch hold of your vision to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you always revealed your transcendental qualities such as compassion, mercy, and magnanimity .

You are the most compassionate, for you preach about Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You are the most magnanimous because you freely distribute the greatest gift-love of Kṛṣṇa, *prema-bhakti.*

May we find shelter in the nectar of your devotional love. We surrender unto you.

*Your servants.*

## Adelaide

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

This day, the appearance day of our spiritual master, is the most important day. We were wandering in this material world in the darkest of ignorance, suffering the threefold miseries, and Śrīla Prabhupāda, out of his causeless mercy, has saved us. There would have been and there is still no hope without his mercy. That mercy manifested before us all as his pastimes of preaching in this world, and now that he has left, that mercy is felt in his remembrance and his transcendental instructions, which are nondifferent from *Bhagavad-gītā.*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have created your wonderful ISKCON society and have made it abundantly clear that by serving and accepting this structure we are always connected with you in a reciprocal way. Therefore on this the commemoration of your appearance before our mundane senses, we fall in the dust of your lotus feet and pray to remain fixed in service within the precepts of your ISKCON society.

*Your servants in Adelaide, Australia,*

## Atlanta

All glory to Śri *Guru* and Gaurāṅga!

Dear *Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet.

A dream. .. .

It was an all-ISKCON festival in the spiritual sky. Everyone was there, and we all knew why-we all knew other and knew that we would never again have to die. Like a huge, ever-expanding *paṇḍāl*, it was "our ISKCON in the spiritual sky," as you had told us, and everyone was there, except of course for Mr. Nair! everyone was ecstatically offering obeisances; now we were more than preaching acquaintances. But you were the one person we were all waiting to see. Over my shoulder I could see the material world with imprisoned catlike *jīvas,* changing forms in a dark jungle mire, and the whole scene was like looking into a blazing fire.

At the front of the hall, way up high, stood a royal *vyāsāsana.* We had all been waiting for this moment, when you would walk through the door. We were all chanting and dancing-the *kīrtana* seemed to be of a different quality. The chanting was overwhelming as we were all spinning and swaying so gracefully, leaping so high and freely, feeling no restriction just a growing, new ecstasy and anticipation of seeing you.

For the first time, we would see you again, *really* see you, with eyes smeared with the ointment of love.

And then you entered, as you had done so many times before: gliding. You walked towards the *vyāsāsana-beaming,* as the sun breaking through clouds, as reality piercing through illusion, light dissipating darkness. You entered the room as you had entered our misguided lives and our desertlike hearts in the past. Finally we were together again, as you had described it. The *kīrtana* roared louder, and we all leaped higher and higher, as you sat upon your *vyāsāsana.*

Off to your right was a multicolored but predominantly blue curtain, gradually, in a poetic, graceful way, it began to slide open. From beyond the curtain came several devotees dancing down marble-crystal stairs. They carried a huge, ever-increasing cake to offer to you. All the devotees were trying to decorate and serve the cake; no one was trying to enjoy the cake. The cake was ISKCON, and on the cake were temples, farms, *gurukulas*, theaters, and musical and other cultural projects.

If you looked inside the little temples on the cake, you could see the devotees in each particular temple down in the material world preaching, chanting and performing their other ISKCON functions.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you always stressed "Work now, *samādhi* later." Small glimpses like these of the *"samādhi later"* help to increase my taste for the "work now." So much work is to be done, and we all are praying for increased ability and your continued loving guidance, so that we may perform all our activities as an offering to you.

*your servants of Atlanta ISKCON and Murāri-sevaka farm.*

## Austria

*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
We simply desire to be your dogs,  
We pray that we may be your menial servants,  
And hope against hope that one day we may please you.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
We wish to be your dogs,  
Running around parking lots and stopping people on the streets,  
Going into offices, factories, hospitals and schools,  
Standing at street lights, riding on buses and residing in airports,  
To distribute your books to the poor and fallen conditioned souls.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You live eternally in your instructions, your books,  
You live in your ISKCON temples and your ISKCON devotees,  
You live in your saṅkīrtana vans and your BBT;  
You live in your farm projects and your future varṇāśrama society.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You live within the hearts of your most sincere disciples, the GBC, and their followers live with you.

*Your servants,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda you will never leave our hearts.  
Our lives are sustained by the sound  
Of your voice, sweet and melodious.  
One can attain perfection by executing any one.  
Of the nine processes of devotional service.  
We pray to attain the perfection  
Of forever hearing your Vaikuṇṭha voice.

Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotees relish inconceivable bliss  
By vibrating the two syllables KṚṢ-ṆA.  
But we. who are sinful and fallen,  
Experience great pleasure by chanting the four syllables  
PRA-BHU-PĀ-DA.  
We pray to attain the perfection  
Of forever chanting your holy name.  
JAYA Śrīla Prabhupāda

Your perfect instructions. your pure example, your sublime pastimes-  
We pray to attain the perfection  
Of always remembering you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We want everyone to read about your nectarean pastimes  
And worship your literary form.  
From your vyāsāsana you inspire us daily as  
We pray to attain the perfection  
Of forever worshiping your deity form.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are praying to you for the strength  
To push on your ISKCON movement.  
Throughout the day we are begging you,  
Please make us suitable instruments for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.  
We pray to attain the perfection  
Of forever offering prayers at your lotus feet.

You taught us life’s perfection.  
Serving Kṛṣṇa in separation.  
For us Kṛṣṇa is far away,  
But your order is within our hearts,  
And seeing Kṛṣṇa is within that order.  
We pray to attain the perfection  
Of becoming your unalloyed servants.

To become Kṛṣṇa as friend in service is an end  
Of perfection in bhakti-yoga.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our ever well-wisher  
Because you introduced us to Kṛṣṇa  
We pray to attain the perfection  
Of gaining your eternal friendship.

Surrendering everything to Kṛṣṇa  
Who but you can claim  
This absolute platform of giving?  
Every day we will try to serve you.  
Until we have given you everything.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray to attain the perfection  
Of surrendering everything at your lotus feet.

*Your servants of New Kulīna-grāma.*

## Bangkok

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet. To find sufficient adjectives to praise you is not difficult. You are most praiseworthy, and your achievements are well known to all. So, by consulting a thesaurus, we could begin to put together a suitable essay.

But we know that literary ingenuity cannot impress you as you read these offerings. You are looking to see how much, year after year, your disciples, granddisciples, and eventually great-granddisciples, are progressing on the path back to Godhead by developing their affection for, and surrender to, your divine lotus feet.

This is our spiritual life. This has meaning. No one but the devotees can understand this sweet exchange between *guru* and disciple. You are the *guru* of *gurus,* the shelter of those who give shelter, the *founder-ācārya* of your own International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

Through the association of devotees in your ISKCON society, our path of spiritual progress is straightforward, pure and simple. We are so much grateful to you for having given us the association of pure devotees in the ISKCON society.

We just want to serve you, following your program of chanting, lancing, feasting and vigorous preaching. Although our tendency is to stray away, the austerities involved in our preaching service force us to always drag our minds back to the shelter of your fearless lotus feet. This is your mercy.

*Janme janme prabhu sei-this* is our greatest hope, to obtain that shelter birth after birth. Empires may come and go, the swirling, twirling illusory energy may present so much phantasmagoria before us but we desire only to remain fixed in meditation on your lotus feet-even through the calamities of death and rebirth. You are the personification of kindness. Who could be kinder than you? So kindly award that benediction to us. We are eternally grateful to you.

*Your servants at ISKCON Bangkok,*

Prabhaviṣṇu Swami

Ātmavidya dāsa / llāpati dāsa / Satyavāk dāsa / Jaya Mādhava dāsa / Khaṭvāṅga dāsa / Nṛsiṁha Tirtha dāsa / Sammohinī-devi dāsi

## Benelux

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet. On this most glorious day of Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, your appearance day, we cannot think of anything else but the remembrance of your wonderful earthly līlā*.* With the passing of the years these pastimes are becoming to us more and more nectarean and precious. This is also true for everything else that you have left us, including the potency to go on and expand. In the material world everything is quickly deteriorating, but you have brought us something that is eternally becoming more and more beautiful, tasty and opulent. We feel so grateful to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for the compassion that you have shown in coming and saving us, the tolerance you have in forgiving our offenses, the patience that you are using to guide us, and the love you are showing us in giving us Kṛṣṇa. We are so happy to be your disciples and grand disciples. We know it to be the greatest position in all the three worlds, because you are the greatest representative of Kṛṣṇa in this whole universe. What you have accomplished has never been achieved by anyone else, as far as we know it from recorded Vedic history. Sometimes we read how great devotees have liberated this or that demon, but you are liberating thousands of them, you being the personality who is fulfilling the desire of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in every town and village. He is the most magnanimous *avatāra* ever descended on earth. His movement is the most amazing display of mercy that has ever taken place in the whole universe; and you are His dearest devotee. You have realized all the imports of Vedic knowledge by knowing Kṛṣṇa and His mission, and therefore you are the one absolute *ācārya* for the age of Kali that for thousands and thousands of years to come will remain as the example to be strictly followed by anyone in the role of bona fide spiritual master.

We feel very safe and secure in your movement, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Every thing here is so nice, unlimitedly blissful, and we don't have any material anxieties or fears. What a rare and most valuable gift is this Kṛṣṇa conscious life-style! What a wonderful association! By your divine grace we sinful and insignificant human beings, lost in the lowest regions , Kali-yuga, had the inconceivable fortune to meet Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the greatest friend and well-wisher of all living entities, who appears only once on this planet after many Kali-yugas. How can we ask you for anything else? With you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are completely satisfied: we think of ourselves as the most fortunate persons that we feel so in debt to you, not knowing how to fully repay you. This is our only thought: day and night we are thanking you thousands and thousands of times. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. When we chant the *mahā-mantra,* we are simply thanking you, Śrīla Prabhupāda; when we dance, it is simply another way to say thank you by expressing our happiness. When we worship the Deity or preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the world, opening temples and making devotees, it is simply to thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda to please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda; to glorify you as the greatest devotee; to somehow try to accomplish this impossible and most blissful task of repaying you.

In the material world a person becomes very happy if he gets something without having to pay, and debts always make one very morose and depressed. To the contrary, we feel completely the opposite. In our case not only have we freely received the greatest of all gifts by getting Kṛṣṇa His service, which has made us unlimitedly happy, but even more than that, the eternal debt of love which we owe you for your mercy and is making us eternally your slaves in your *saṅkīrtana* movement is of even greater satisfaction-the most fortunate position and your greatest gift.

Although you gave us everything possibly imaginable (and so praying for something would seem ridiculous), we still have one intense longing our hearts: "Our most beloved *Gurudeva*, please keep us always as a particle of dust at your divine lotus feet." We want to keep walking with you all over the planet for as many thousands of years as you will decide on to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You have shown us in just a few the greatest spectacle of the most wonderful display of devotional opulences, in the form of thousands of pastimes, ever seen by ordinary *jīvas* like us. Because of this, we have now become addicted to being with and we are eagerly waiting to see from you millions of other miracles. We want to see how, by the strength of your devotion and compassion, you will make Lord Caitanya become visibly manifest as the Supreme Lord of Kali-*yuga*, how you will make the whole world Kṛṣṇa conscious, the entire planet chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa surrendered at His feet and going back to Godhead. We have no other concept of spiritual life besides this vision ; we do not know any other meaning to the word *samādhi;* we do not aspire for any other kind of happiness or goal in life other than to participate in this mission. When we hear the word liberation*,* we cannot think of anything other than serving you. The greatest calamity-even greater than having to take lower births for unlimited lifetimes-would be to flyaway from your lotus feet. We have already tolerated for so long all kinds of suffering in all types of bodies, and we can go on tolerating them again; but we would not be able to tolerate being separated from you. We cannot even think of it, it is so painful. Just as for a materialistic person attached to sense gratification it is very difficult to think of death, because that would mean the end of his material pleasure and personality, and to think of total annihilation would be impossible, for us it is similarly inconceivable to imagine spiritual life without you, or to be with Kṛṣṇa without you, or chanting, dancing, or feasting without you, or anything else in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You have taught us how to love Kṛṣṇa and to be attached to Him, but we have also realized that Kṛṣṇa is your property. We will never give up Kṛṣṇa consciousness! We cannot give it up; we are too attached to it. So how can we even conceive of leaving you? You are the best teacher and friend, and under your guidance and affection we have learned our lesson very well. When Lord Caitanya is saying in *Śikṣāṣṭakam* that He is not attracted, it is only in the highest sense of the term, for a devotee at the stage of perfection of love is always aspiring for more service and feels always dissatisfied, thinking that he is not doing enough to please Kṛṣṇa, and therefore he considers himself fallen. But if we would say that we are not attached, and as a consequence leave Kṛṣṇa consciousness, that would be like saying that Śrīla Prabhupāda did not teach us well enough, or that his books are not sufficiently comprehensive, or that his movement or his disciples are not good enough association. This would be the greatest insult to your supremely loving personality; it would be the most degraded and envious of all types of mentality. We know for certain, without the slightest doubt (and you have proved it by giving us Kṛṣṇa), that you are the best spiritual master. How can we maintain our life without you? If somebody would ask us, "Why are you dressed like this? Why do you have that tuft of hair? Why are you doing like this or that?" or "Who are you?" we would simply answer, "Because Śrīla Prabhupāda did it, because Śrīla Prabhupāda told us, because Śrīla Prabhupāda likes it, and .we are simply the servants of the servants of the servants of His Divine Grace Jagad-*guru*, ISKCON *founder-āśārya,* savior of the universe, Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda, our beloved spiritual master. " Millions of glories to you and your movement from your faithful and enthusiastic, but insignificant, servants of the Benelux Yātrā.

*Your disciples and granddisciples,*

**Rādhādeśa**

## Bhaktivedanta Ashram (Sydney)

**Śrī guru-praṇāma**

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, who has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge.**

**Remembering Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Glowworms in darkness shine,  
Appearing to give light  
In the age called Kali-yuga.

For nondevotees,  
Black is white,  
Night-day  
Distress their happiness.  
Bereft of sunshine  
Emanating from you,  
They remain in illusion,  
Suffering threefold misery,  
Material pangs,  
Loss of blood-sex,  
Loss of brain-intoxication,  
Killing-eating flesh and fat-no mercy,  
Gambling-useless daydream-no humility,  
That we are God,  
All is one  
Appears fun  
Enjoyment in the age called Kali-yuga.

Your glories are magnificent,  
Your self-determination,  
Bold courage-might.  
You dissipate  
From our sight.  
Saving all who would rot  
In world of matter,  
Death with no hope.

Impossible to repay, !  
Please accept my services,  
Now and beyond the end of time.

Kindly benedict me  
With memories of you,  
Smiling-laughing-joking,  
Grave-serious,  
Casual-loving-caring,  
Selfless,  
Ever well-wisher,  
Father of mankind,  
Perfect pure,  
Inebriety-free.  
Let me remember.

Inconceivable appearance,  
Bold philosopher-meek,  
Unconquerable-gentle,  
Intelligent-simple,  
Dexterous-no duplicity,  
Straight forward-mild,  
No pretension-all appreciated,  
Transcendental,  
Loved by all,  
"that which is not" You saved my life.  
Let me remember you.

## Bhaktivedanta Manor

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this most auspicious occasion of your blessed appearance, we cast our minds back six years to your Vyāsa-pūjā of 1977. You made a final visit in *vapuḥ* form to the Western world and spent eight days at Bhaktivedanta Manor, thus mercifully affording us your

precious association. Although your body was ravaged with illness, your pastimes revealed the transcendental nature of your life and mission.

The plane journey from India was long and made more arduous by the six-hour holdup in Rome. Throughout the flight you remained silent and grave. Coming into London, your secretary, Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami, concerned that you may be suffering severe discomfort, offered encouraging words: "Prabhupāda, when you get to London the devotees will be so pleased to see you."

You replied, "Yes, it is good that we have come." How can we ever appreciate your total sacrifice on our behalf? And how is it that one who is the most intimate friend of the Lord should suffer for the sake of inspiring and saving such unworthy souls? Your compassion carried you above all mundane inconvenience. Invalid on your bed, you spoke of the people of London: "They do not know how *they* are suffering."

When you arrived, you took a small sweet, and we were all happy that for the first time in weeks you had eaten. But afterwards you ate nothing solid. Then one morning you urgently called the senior devotees to your room. With fearful apprehension they awaited your words… Are the devotees getting nice *prāsādam?"* There has never been such a father and well-wisher of his helpless children as you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

You came down to the temple room each morning to attend the *kīrtana,* but you requested that we not sing "*Śrī Guru-caraṇa-padma*" in your presence, since the intense emotions you experienced from the love of your devotees was overpowering to your weak body. Every movement of body and face was inconceivably painful; still your love compelled you inspire us. During *kīrtana* you gently tapped your fingers onto the palm of your other hand; immediately the *kīrtana* exploded with the beat of the *mṛdaṅgas* and clapping. Then you made an upward motion of your palm; the devotees, intent on your every movement, leapt to the ceiling . Seei*ng* our antics, a slight smile broke on your lips, and our hearts melted, filled with the vow that all we ever want to do is somehow please you

We carried you by palanquin to your room. At the top of the stairs it was turned so that you could see the devotees below. In humble gratitude you raised your hands together, paying respects to your disciples, thanking us for taking an interest in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

All too soon you returned to India and to Kṛṣṇa. You gave everything to us; how could we ever become ungrateful and let the great enemies lethargy and complacency weaken our resolve? We pray to develop your mood of sacrifice, of compassion, of concern and intense spiritual love among ourselves and for all souls who are still waiting to be delivered by your divine mercy.

*We beg to remain your obedient servants,*

*The devotees of Bhaktivedanta Manor.*

## Bhaktivedanta Village

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Service is the life and soul of the devotees, the life and soul of our movement. By your kindness you have given lifetimes of devotional service to perform. There is so much to be done and perhaps so little time to do it. While my mind can seldom think beyond today's

meager tasks, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, envisioned great projects, a worldwide preaching movement, the deliverance of all living entities in the universe. You are fully capable of singlehandedly making this planet Kṛṣṇa conscious, but you have shared this burden with your disciples just for our purification and benefit.

So, our life of service is a busy one. Every day is filled with the work of devotional service: the preaching programs must go on, the children must be taught, the Deity must be served nicely, the gardens must be planted. But we must also remember why we are working and whom we are serving. Actually, it is only by your desire that this entire movement is propelled onward and by your instruction that we are engaging in all varieties of devotional service.

On this Vyāsa-pūjā day we try to especially remember you, your glorious pastimes, your perfect qualities, and our relationship with you. While this auspicious day is particularly significant, our remembrance of you should be not only this day or even just every day, but at every moment of our lives! We are convinced that if we can simply remember you and attempt to carry out your orders, then our lives are sure to be successful.

We must remember all you have done for this material world, filled with the darkest ignorance of our Kali-yuga culture. You have begun a universal movement of genuine spiritual life that has spread to every continent and practically to every country. You have made the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa known everywhere. Who can count the millions of lives eternally touched by the pure sound vibration of the mahā-mantra? Your literary accomplishments have been lauded by devotees and scholars alike. Your sixty volumes of Vedic literature will be read and re-read by millions for years and years to come.

Your message is not mere theory, but a practical program for revitalizating our decaying, hedonistic culture and establishing true Vaiṣṇava principles and practice. You accomplished this through temples, farms, schools , restaurants, book production, art, music, drama; an entire array of devotional engagements. Perhaps the world does not yet fully appreciate what you have given, but some day history will record those few years you were with us as the turning point for human civilization and the salvation of a world bent on self-destruction.

We must also remember what you done for each one of us individually as your disciples. Sometimes it is difficult to fully grasp the universal scope of your accomplishments, but we all know in our hearts what you have done for each of us! All good qualities were absent in our lives. Religious principles were not even considered. We were either addicted to mindless sense gratification that could never satisfy the real cravings of the soul, or we were filled with the anxiety of doubt, despair and depression. There were few of us with ‘bright futures’ ahead, and that brightness was only measured by the shallow and temporary yardstick of material success and happiness.

Into this land of atheism and alienation you came, bringing a philosophy so sound, so scientific, that it attracted our cynical minds and intelligence, a devotion so deep and pure that it won over our hardened hearts! That relief of mind and heart was not another fad or hobby or interest that would soon wane, but the beginning of true understanding, true purification and finally true commitment to the cause of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

But there is no meaning in service to or love of God without service to or love for the spiritual master. Gradually, we are learning to love Kṛṣṇa, but it is you, the *guru*, who have taught us and guided us and given us that precious link with Kṛṣṇa. Without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we would never know Kṛṣṇa. Without knowing Kṛṣṇa, we would never be able to serve Him, chant His name, offer *prasādam* and, ultimately, give up our entire lives to His service.

So we must remember that whatever small service we are fortunate to perform, whatever small faith we have somehow acquired, whatever small attraction we find in our hearts, whatever small detachment from materiallife we are developing, has come about only by your causeless mercy upon us. Therefore, we most humbly beg and pray that we might remain lifetime after lifetime your most faithful servants!

*From the devotees of Bhaktivedanta Village,*

## Binghamton

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**"I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism."**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Before you came to spread the message of Lord Caitanya in these Western countries, we were completely lost in the scorching desert of impersonalism and voidistic speculation. The modern so-called civilization is based on the demoniac principle that "this World is unreal, that there is no foundation, and that there is no God in control *(Bhagavad-gītā* 16.8) In the name of rational scientific knowledge, this demoniac, voidistic principle has been established as the foundation of all our thinking, and it has been advanced by the prominent leaders of Western society as the only source of direction and meaning in our lives. As a consequence, our lives had no meaning or direction before you came. Our only source of direction was the prompting of our gross material senses, and thus we, along with this entire society, were simply sliding deeper and deeper into a black pit of degradation.

In this horrible and dangerous condition; we could never have saved ourselves by the insignificant powers of our conditioned material minds and senses. However, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have come, out of your unlimited mercy and compassion, to save us. You have brought us the message that the basis of reality is an absolute sentient person the original Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You have taught us that we ourselves are not merely animated lumps of matter. Rather, we are eternal spirit souls, and as spiritual beings we have an eternal relationship with the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa Himself. This knowledge is not simply another dry speculative theory. Rather, you have shown us the practical science of God-realization, whereby each individual person can actually reawaken his eternal relationship with Kṛṣṇa and thereby find the true purpose and meaning of his life. By doing this you have not merely saved us from the path of material degradation rather, you have overflooded the sterile desert of our material lives with an ocean of transcendental joy that we could never have even anticipated, much less realized. This is entirely due to your causeless mercy, and because of this we are eternally indebted to you.

Since you are a pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa, you are by nature filled with compassion for all of the fallen spirit souls, and you have undergone great personal suffering in an effort to save them. You have always been particularly concerned about the plight of those spirit souls who are being dragged further and further into illusion by the atheistic speculative theories of modern science. Your concern for the suffering caused by these false theories is shown by your instructions to Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Mahāraja in 1973: "Because they \[the scientists\] are standing on the wrong theory, all their calculations are wrong, and people are suffering. The rascal Darwin's theory-based on this foolish theory, so many have the wrong conception of life. So we have to challenge, protest, defeat; this will be our work. Our worshiping of Kṛṣṇa-that is our internal affair; the external affair will be to establish that life does not come from matter; matter comes from life. Otherwise they will be misleading the society. ...You protest. Write books! You area scientist, so prove it scientifically. That will be your laurel of taking the doctorate degree."

In your desire to save people from the voidistic speculations of modern science, you have never taken the kind of negative, destructive attitude that is sometimes displayed by the followers of narrow, sectarian religions. Rather, as a true *mahātmā,* or broad minded person, you have taught us that all valid activities become perfect when they are used in the service of Kṛṣṇa. As you pointed out in Ś*rīmad-Bhāgavatam,* "Science and philosophy should be applied in the service of the Lord. There is no use in presenting dry speculative theories for sense gratification. Philosophy and science should be engaged to establish the glory of the Lord. Advanced people are eager to understand the Absolute Truth through the medium of science, and therefore a great scientist should endeavor to prove the existence of the Lord on a scientific basis." *(Bhāg.* 1.5.22purport)

By giving us these and many other instructions on how to spread the transcendental knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you have given us more than a lifetime's engagement in service at your lotus feet and the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. We must take these instructions as our life and soul, even though we are completely unworthy and unqualified. We can only pray that you will continue to benedict us with your mercy so that we may be able to eternally engage in your service.

*Your worthless servants,*  
Sadāpūta dāsa / Keśihantā dāsa

## Bogotā

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*śrī-caitanya-mano-'bhīṣṭaṁ sthāpitaṁ yena bhū-tale*  
*svayaṁ rūpaḥ kadā mahyaṁ dadāti sva-padāntikamnama*

*oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We would like to join together with all of our Godbrothers from all over the world on this auspicious day of Vyāsa-pūjā to offer our most humble obeisances to the dust of your wonderful lotus feet.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, how will it ever be possible to understand the mercy and kindness you have showered on such wretched, undeserving souls such as ourselves? Even more than that, how will it be possible to ever repay you for this causeless mercy? You overlooked an ocean of bad qualities to find some tiny spark of interest in spiritual life in our darkened hearts. The Ś*rīmad-Bhāgavatam* describes this as the quality of the most exalted souls, that they can overlook the faults in someone and find some good quality in them and increase that quality. Just like now we have seen your biography film with a little footage of the early days in Tompkins Square Park and on 26 Second Avenue. We see you there with so many people that now we think are just a bunch of crazy nuts with whom we would never associate. But there you are with them; and like a truly great soul you ignore their bad qualities and in your infinite mercy and humility you take that almost invisible spark and fan it into the fire of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. That is your incredible mercy, that you took this wretched band of hippies and made them into a powerful army of preachers. Now we see these same "crazy nuts" have organized such a marvelous preaching mission all over the world, beautifully illustrated books in almost every language, exquisite temples, restaurants, farms, television and radio shows, dioramas, ...O Śrīla Prabhupāda, only you, who are truly a touchstone, could make this transformation, see some tiny good quality and magnify it. When will that day come when we will really be able to appreciate who you are?

And again I ask, O Prabhupāda, how can we repay you? But you have given us the answer: PREACH! PREACH! And to maintain an effective preaching mission we need to cooperate. As you said, "Your love for me will be shown by the way you cooperate among yourselves when I am gone." So let us take this opportunity to meditate a moment on your transcendental vision of seeing the good qualities in others and beg from you that we can always appreciate all of your wonderful servants. And thus filled with this spirit of cooperation, we can go out and inundate all the corners of the universe with your mandate: "Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and be happy!"

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
*Your humble servants in Bogotā, Colombia*

## Bombay

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this auspicious day in honor of your appearance, we devotees of Bombay temple pray to always remember your glorious activities here at Hare Krishna Land and to follow in your footsteps to develop this project. As the representative of Kṛṣṇa, you not only accept or and praise, but you also accepted so many difficulties on behalf Kṛṣṇa in your fight for this land. We worship you and we are indebted for the many gifts you have given us. As you said in a lecture here in 1977,*"Guru-pūjā* is not cheap adoration. It is the process of enlightening, *divya-jñāna.* Because the *guru* gives *divya-jñāna,* he is worshiped."

When you first came to Juhu you prayed to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsavihāri to come to this dusty field and promised to build Them a palace if They would kindly stay here. In the same way, you begged the Western devotees, who were reluctant, to come and live on the land in the hut, suffering the onslaughts of the elements, disease and the municipality, promising them purification. You always had wonderful plans for Bombay, and you revealed them even before seeing the Juhu land. As you stated in your letter to the first life members, (*Śrīla Prabhupāda Lila*, V 01.4): "My plan is to establish a unique International Krishna Conscious Training Centre, where hundreds of persons from abroad may be educated in the Vedic way of life, while at the same time Indian boys and girls may be trained up for *prachar* (preaching) work in foreign countries."

For the execution of your plans you gave us many instructions, as on one morning walk, when you explained to us the meaning of the words ā*ra na kariyā mane āśāi.* You said that there should be no diversion of attention, no other desire than to carry out the orders of the spiritual master. "If you make your own plan, that is impure." And in every step of the plan you gave us your guidance. When one devotee informed you that there would be a place for a resident doctor on the land, you said, "This temple is meant for curing the material disease, *bhava-roga,* not this headache and stomachache." You would always keep us strictly to the principle of *paropakāra.* especially here in India. You would never tire of quoting:

*Bhārata bhūmite haila manuṣya janma jāra*  
*Janma sārthaka kari karo para-upakāra*

**"First you know Kṛṣṇa, then you tell others about Kṛṣṇa."**

You would never let us deviate from this highest welfare work.

Since India is the seat of Māyāvada philosophy and there are endless variations of it to be found, you also gave us all the arguments to defeat their nonsense philosophical speculations. On one morning walk Girirāja Swami asked you a question that had been asked of him at one program. That is, If Lord Rāma is supreme, then why did He worship Lord Śiva in the *Rāmāyaṇa?* You answered that just as Kṛṣṇa a likes to be chastised by mother Yaśoda, so Lord Rāma likes to worship Lord Śiva. Kṛṣṇa a likes to glorify His devotees. One devotee then said that one worshiper of Śiva had given the explanation that in worshiping Śiva, Lord Rāma was asking Lord Śiva's permission to kill Rāvaṇa, who was Lord Śiva's devotee. At that you said that this devotee of Śiva was saying that Lord Śiva is a rascal, because only a rascal would give permission to someone to kill his son or devotee. In this way, you very easily and soundly defeated all arguments.

You also liked to walk on Juhu Beach with Dr. Patel and his friends, and you would sometimes chastise them, saying, " Anyone who is not Kṛṣṇa a conscious is a *mūḍha."* And when Dr. Patel would protest, you would reply innocently that you were not saying this but Kṛṣṇa was saying it in *Bhagavad-gitā: na māṁ duṣkṛtino mūḍhāḥ.* You would give Dr. Patel instructions, as you did to everyone, to preach. Once you revealed to him the formula of how to preach according to Prabodhānanda Sarasvatī. "First you flatter the man, then fall at his feet and beg one thing: 'Please forget all your rascaldom, whatever you have learned, and worship the lotus feet of Lord Caitanya.' If you want to be recognized by Kṛṣṇa, just follow this process."

You also showed him your quality of humility and strict adherence to *the paramparā* system. Once Dr. Patel said, half-jokingly, "I have read your commentary on *Bhagavad-gitā* and Rāmānuj*ācārya*'s, and they are absolutely parallel. I think you have taken something out of it." But you seriously replied, "Our process is to take from the *aśāryas.* We are not so fools and rascals that we have to manufacture. We have to take the remnants of the foodstuffs given by the *aśāryas* and explain in the modern way. That is our business. If it is parallel, then that is my success, and if it is not parallel, then it is useless."

You also showed your firm determination to fight to the end with the demons for this land. In one lecture here on Lord Nṛsiṁha's appearance day you described the constant struggle between the atheists and the theists and said, "The atheists and theists will never agree, but we will never submit to the atheists." However, you did not take the credit for the victory, but always remained dependent on Kṛṣṇa. One devotee once asked you if it was all right to pray to Kṛṣṇa for assistance in his service to the spiritual master. You replied, "Yes, that is all right. Just like in the construction of this Bombay temple, there was so much obstruction. So we prayed to Kṛṣṇa for protection. But that is for Kṛṣṇa's purpose."

Yet, when the battle was over and the land was won, you did not acknowledge any difficulty. Sometime later in one class in Europe you were explaining how in Kṛṣṇa consciousness there are no problems, and you gave this Bombay land as an example. You described it as a paradise, with palm trees and many buildings, saying how valuable a property it was, but how cheaply you had purchased it. In your transcendental vision there was no problem. We devotees of Bombay temple are just praying at your lotus feet to always remember your pastimes here and desiring your blessings to fulfill your transcendental vision of this temple as a center to attract all people to loving service at the lotus feet of Śri Śri Rādhā-Rāsavihārī.

*Your servants, the devotees of Bombay temple.*

## Boston

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you on this observance of Vyāsa-pūjā. On this special day we are trying to recapture treasured memories of you, our beloved master, who are delivering to us the treasure of Kṛṣṇa-bhakti.

We remember you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as the one whose spiritual potency penetrated the coverings of ignorance and passion, revealing to us a glimpse of spiritual truth. *Jīvera svarūpa haya kṛṣṇa nitya dāsa: we* are not these bodies, we are eternal servants of God. So powerful was this experience that it has left an indelible impression on our minds. Your association has ignited a spark of *prema* within our hearts, steadily being fanned into a blazing fire of love of Kṛṣṇa by the continuous waves of your mercy.

We remember how, although you were very happy in Vṛndāvana, you left. Accepting great inconvenience and austerity, you struggled to establish your ISKCON movement. You gave your life to the service of Śri Caitanya Mahāprabhu, which made for our being given life. ..eternal life in devotional service.

We remember that your most cherished desire was to see ISKCON spread all over the universe. One time you revealed to an acquaintance the future success of ISKCON. You said, "1 am not a poor man, I am rich. There are temples and books, they are existing, they are there, but the time is separating us from them." This was all to be realized later. Similarly, we may say that the whole universe is liberated, and chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa is already existing, just separated by time. And in time this will also be revealed as your glorious preaching activities manifest throughout the universe.

We remember your kind and expert guidance. Like the captain of a ship, you steered us away from the insidious allurements of the illusory energy and are navigating us through the nectarean ocean of devotional service. As Kṛṣṇa's transcendental ambassador, representing the complete manifestation of His mercy, you are reclaiming all the fallen souls.

But most of all, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we remember you as the one who came to us in our darkest hour, when we were plunged in the ignorance of gross materialism, suffering a meaningless and hopeless existence. Yes, you came like an illuminating beacon, dispelling the confusion and darkness, revealing the Absolute Truth: eternal happiness and love in the auspicious service of Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, these fond remembrances of you awaken loving ecstasy within us. Please let the impression of your sublime personality and transcendental characteristics remain forever imprinted in our hearts. You are the most wonderful jewel this world has ever known, and now, in your absence, this world is bereft of its most valuable jewel. Therefore, in separation from you we are living only to serve your mission. By being absorbed in your service, we are hankering only for that time when we can again bathe in the soothing, moonlike rays of your compassionate glance.

*Your servants in New England.*

**Boston Yātrā**

## Brisbane

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Only by your mercy and compassion can we, the most fallen personalities, attribute whatever little success we have achieved with our lives. Like clumsy, awkward children we are learning how to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. For you it was so spontaneous-like a swan, which is graceful by nature, you had the perfect answer for every situation. You so skilfully implemented love of Godhead into every strong-hold of Kali-yuga, from New York down to every major city in the world-a feat so miraculous that so many conditioned souls who were wallowing in the pits of nescience could be able to grasp the topmost principles of human life and take to devotional service.

We have learned from you about the nature of the conditioned soul and *saṁṣāra.* Just as the four seasons rotate annually on the wheel of time, there is a similar movement on a more massive scale, namely, the cycle of *yugas-Satya,* Dvāpara, Tretā and Kali.

As one naturally seeks shelter from heavy torrents of the rainy season, one also has to search for deliverance from the heavy onslaught of the material energy. In Satya-yuga great sages would perform *tapasya* for thousands of years by meditating on the Supreme Lord. In Tretā-yuga pious souls such as Mahārāja Ambarīṣa would focus all their attention on supra-excellent standards of deity worship. In Dvāpara-yuga there were fire sacrifices-Mahārāja Yudhiṣṭhira satisfied Kṛṣṇa with his Rājasūya sacrifice.

But now, in the age of Kali, or quarrel, all spiritual vision has been swept aside by dualities of desire and hate. Many cheaters have sprung up to mislead the people who are looking for a spiritual path. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have carried Lord Caitanya's banner all over the world and offered this rare and precious gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Under the shelter of the umbrella of the ISKCON movement you have given us protection from the age of Kali, just as Kṛṣṇa protected the inhabitants of Vṛīndavana from the deluge of *samvartaka* rain clouds. There is no other way out of this material entanglement. How can we ever repay you!

*Your most unworthy servants in Brisbane and Surfers Paradise Yātrās,*

## Caitanya College

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you. Last year in our offering from Caitanya College, we regretted the disastrous events of the year and the culmination of them in the loss of so many devotees. As the English Yatra was rocked by the events, many programs were threatened; but we were determined to maintain and increase the standard above that of before. So here is a short report of last year's development, to complete the promise we made a year ago.

**Book Distribution**-Last year our book distribution was hampered because we needed all our *saṅkīrtana* party on hand to collect to maintain the *yātrā.* After the loss of *saṅkīrtana* devotees (more than ha force), maintenance was the first consideration. So all in all we distributed about forty thousand Ś*rimad-Bhāgavatams* in the year, which is about ten percent of the previous year's score. Still, we did not printing, but instead we printed 170,000 hardbound books (First Canto Ś*rimad Bhāgavatam)* for future distribution.

**Devotee-Making**-The 110 devotees that we lost last year have now been replaced in one year. About forty of them returned, and the balance is new devotees that were made. In fact, the caliber of our new men is better than ever. This very moment we have over thirty devotees *bhakta* program.

**Deity Worship**-Some offerings and *pūjā* were missed in the heat crisis last spring, but the Deities are now being worshiped better than before. Your favorite Deities, Rādhā-Londonīśvara and Rādhā- Gokulānanda, are more beautiful than ever before, and this year we have standardized all the *pūjā* in the *yātrā .* All the worship is perform *brāhmaṇas.*

**Maintenance** -Śrīla Prabhupāda, you will be glad to know that , buildings were kept. None were sold (as some suggested), and actually the standard has been improved. In Bhaktivedanta Manor we built new ladies' *prasādam* rooms, renovated the reception room, men's *prasādam* room and V. I. P. dining room, and a new central heater was install Caitanya College the entire ground floor is being renovated as a museum, with historical, cultural, philosophical, audiovisual displays and dioramas, as well as a museum of Your Divine Grace, a restaurant and With a little local advertising we received three hundred guests on Sunday. So we expect to get that many a day, paying one and pounds each, upon the opening of the museum on June 1st.

**Preaching** -Our Friends of Lord Krishna program is really booming, and we have regular preaching engagements at the Manor for all sorts of guests, from upper to middle class. This year Śrila Bhagavān Mahārāja met the Pope, the Dean of Westminster , many dignitaries, millionaires, movie producers, etc. Our public image was very much enhanced by some TV shows, as well as last year's Festival of India, a joint British- Indian cultural project of which we were a part. There were also seven Ratha-yātrās across the country, including London, Birmingham and Glasgow.

But above all, the consciousness of the devotees is much improved and actually better than we have ever seen before. Everyone is dedicated to pushing on ISKCON with great enthusiasm, and we are looking forward to publishing ten thousand sets of Ś*rimad-Bhāgavatam* this year.

All this has been possible by the expert guidance and leadership of your great disciple Śrīla Bhāgavan Goswami, who inspired the *yātrā* with a vision of expansion at a time when we doubted that we could even maintain. It is all his credit that the above-mentioned projects developed the way they did.

We have great hope for the future, and we shall make all attempts to offer you better results yearly. We are determined, for this ordeal has just increased our faith and conviction in your instructions and this ISKCON movement. The lessons of independence and lack of cooperation have been learned. Sobered and chastised, we feel purified, realizing the spiritual nature of the movement and the mercy which sustains it. Who would have thought to lose a GBC, *guru,* one third of the *yātrā* and more, and still be able to erase the tide and increase the preaching? It is only possible because you are guiding and maintaining those who follow you strictly. We are more confident than ever of your presence, and with straw in our mouths we offer our respectful obeisances to you again and again on this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day.

*Your servants,*

Śivarāma Swami / Aṅkottha dāsa / Gokulānanda dāsa / Ananta-puruṣa dāsa / Dina-dayārdra dāsa / Bhāgvatśraya dāsa / Sampradāya dāsa / Ādi-kartā dāsa / Udayānanda dāsa / Ekādaśi dāsa / *Ācārya*-seva dāsa / Arjuna-Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Akiyśana dāsa / Caitanyadeva dāsa / Dharmāśraya dāsa */* Yadurāja dāsa / Govinda-bhāṣya dāsa / Kiśora-Kiśorī dāsa / Kṛṣṇa-dharma dāsa / Yamunānātha dāsa / Kṛpāmaya dāsa / Nakula dāsa / Kṛpā-sindhu dāsa / Rāma-Nṛsiṁha dāsa / Paravidya dāsa / Stoka-Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Animiṣa dāsa / *Kṛṣṇanda* dāsa / Girigovardhana dāsa / Hari-kīrtana dāsa / Bhārata dāsa / Bhakta Leroy / Bhakta Adrian / Bhakta Chris / Bhakta Colin / Bhakta Ian / Bhakta Frank / Gopīmātā-devī dāsi / *Guru*- caraṇa-padma-devī dāsi */* Kāmadhuk-devī dāsi / Kṛṣṇa-śakti-devī dās

## Chicago

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

The day you came into the West  
Our meaningless lives were truly blessed.  
Of many jewels you are the crest.  
Of pure devotees you are the best.

You came into this blazing fire  
To deliver us all from illusions mire.  
So many people you did inspire;  
To serve your guru was your only desire.

Coming to a land full of greed and lust  
You could see Kṛṣṇa consciousness was a must.  
Material enjoyment is like a speck of dust;  
Its here for just a moment then gone in a gust.

Material life is simply dry and plain;  
Remaining in it one surely goes insane.  
Devotional service is the greatest gain.  
In its presence nescience can no longer remain.

"Don't waste your lives in such a useless way.  
Your illusion will increase more day by day.  
Please just listen to what I have to say. "  
And then to Lord Kṛṣṇa you began to pray:

"My dear Lord Kṛṣṇa, please hear my prayer.  
Your illusory energy has spread everywhere.  
I'm coming to You in great despair.  
To preach Your message here, who would dare?

"How will they all begin to understand  
This message from a foreign land?  
Māyā has them all dancing in her hand,  
And myself I feel as though I'm a grain of sand.

"I don't know why You have brought me here,  
In a place filled with violence. terror and fear.  
Your transcendental message they do not hear.  
Where am I to find a submissive ear?

"But Your words in the scripture are most certainly true.  
One simply has to hear just only a few,  
And then his heart will turn, afresh and new,  
Just like the early summer morning dew.

"All of these souls are here by Your grace.  
They can only be delivered by Your grace.  
So kindly set them free from this ghastly place.  
Please empower me to preach to this fallen race.

"I have no love nor any devotion.  
How they will be saved, I haven't the notion.  
Help me preach amidst this great commotion.  
Please let them all swim in Lord Caitanya’s ocean. "

After praying to Kṛṣṇa in this humble way,  
You chanted in the park, day after day.  
You didn't even have a decent place to stay,  
But finally some came to hear what you had to say.

And now you are preaching in every place,  
Bringing spiritual wisdom to the human race.  
Material enjoyment is a true disgrace.  
At the thought of it you grimace your face.

You've done what no one else has ever done.  
A worldwide movement you have begun.  
You showed how life can be simply fun.  
You're rounding up the jīvas one by one.

Establishing many temples, farms and schools,  
Preaching to the scholars, you make them look like fools.  
The scientists are just a pack of braying mules.  
In this way you battle in so many duels.

*You have built a house the whole world can share,*  
*And this achievement is truly rare.*  
*To it nothing else can even compare.*  
*You 're preaching the glories of the divine pair.*

You've ushered in a devotional tide.  
All the non devotees you certainly deride.  
Upon seeing you, the impersonalists hide.  
You're preaching the bhakti cult far and wide.

You're saving us all from māyā’s spell.  
You 're saving us all from māyā s foul smell.  
You're saving us all from māyā's dark well.  
You're saving us all from going to hell.

On the occasion of this Vyāsa-pūjā day  
We offer you ārati, and we constantly pray  
That in this great movement we all will stay,  
And that none of us will ever go astray.

First we offer incense that smells really sweet,  
Only to remind us of your divine lotus feet.  
Then we offer you a lamp of fire  
And pray for a blazing pure desire.

Then we offer water above your head  
And pray that our sinful deeds will drown 'til dead.  
Then a cloth is offered in many graceful motions.  
Please dry up our hearts of any lusty notions.

Then unto your feet we offer a flower  
And pray you grant us some spiritual power.  
Then comes the whisk in a gentle way.  
We hope that in our hearts the dust is whisked away.

We're spiritually deaf, dumb. blind and lame.  
We're tired of losing in māyā's game.  
We've simply forgotten life's ultimate aim.  
In your presence we bow our heads in shame.

Please. Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to preach.  
Every man, woman and child your books must reach.  
The principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness we must teach,  
So you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we strongly beseech.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*  
*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda !!!

*Your servants at Chicago temple,*

## Costa Rica

*Our Lord and master Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Millions of glories to you, light of mercy to the fallen souls. We beg you to accept our fallen *daṇḍavats at* your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, friend of the poor, we are in despair. The soothing light of your personal guidance has left us since you went to Kṛṣṇa. We have but a drop of real love for , Why were we so unfortunate not to take full advantage of your presence while you resided amongst us? They say, Better late than never. Here we are again, pleading at your lotus feet, year after year. When can we stand here before you with clean hearts? We are supposed to glorify you, but as always, when we approach you we end up being beggars for mercy.

That is your real glory. Your mercy was there for everyone. We are still begging for that mercy. Without you and without your ISKCON we are feathers in the wind of illusion. Please let us chant eternally, " Jaya Prabhupāda, Jaya Prabhupāda, Jaya Prabhupāda, Jaya Prabhupāda.” Millions of words cannot say more than " Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda.'

You won our hearts. One smile of yours and we had a transcendental home. One plan of yours and we had an eternal service. One book of yours and our petty fighting was finished. One book of yours millions are benedicted. One order from you and sectarianism was transcended. All that, we can never forget. Let us reflect on your wonderful qualities, so that your mission will be fulfilled.

*Thus we remain until the next year, your servants in Costa Rica,*

## Dallas

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Like Parīkṣit Mahārāja, you heard so keenly that your first meeting, Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Mahārāja recognized, "That boy hears very nicely."

Like Śukadeva Gosvāmi, you chanted Ś*rimad Bhāgavatam* with perfect clarity, while your doctors reported that your condition should be one of unbearable, excruciating pain; and you illuminated the Kali-yuga for the next ten thousand years by your Bhaktivedanta purports.

Like Prahlāda Mahārāja, you remembered the Lord in the Bow surrounded by vicious drug addicts and madmen who sometimes tried to kill you.

Like Lakṣmi, you served the lotus feet of the Lord, distributing *caraṇāmṛta* to the fallen souls of every race and continent.

Like Ambarīṣa Mahārāja, you worshiped the Deity, invoking Lord's appearance in hundreds of temples by your pure devotion.

Like Akrūra, you prayed to the Lord to kindly accept the service of your disciples and purify them.

Like Hanumān, you served the Lord, destroying the civilization of the modern-day Rāvaṇas and liberating Lakṣmī from their clutches, using an army of defectors you claimed from their own ranks, sometimes engaging monkeylike persons like myself as your soldiers.

Like Arjuna, you befriended the Lord, who appeared to you dream after your second heart attack aboard the *Jaladuta,* person guiding your ship through the insurmountable storm of Kali-yuga grand success triumphant for the *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Like Bali Mahārāja, you surrendered everything-including wife, family, wealth, and even a spiritually comfortable situation at Rādhā-Dāmodara temple in Vṛndāvana-to fulfill the order of ɱ *Guru* Mahārāja.

On this most sacred of days, please bless us with the nine kind devotional service to your lotus feet, as you are the personification o aspects of pure devotional service.

*Your servants,*

## Denver

"The spiritual master is receiving benediction from the ocean of mercy. Just as a cloud pours water on the forest fire to extinguish it, so the spiritual master extinguishes the blazing fire of material existence. I offer my most humble obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master."

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

The great poet Narottama dāsa has sung, ś*ri-guru- caraṇe rali, ei sei uttama* gati:attachment to the lotus feet of one's *guru* is the perfection which fulfills all desires. How is it that we, who are very expert at sinning, have been given this chance to attain the perfection of self-realization? This is your causeless mercy upon us. By your grace you travelled everywhere to benedict the most fallen souls of this age of Kali. The results themselves are apparent. Wherever your lotus feet touched the ground, devotees came forth and temples were constructed. But for yourself, we could find no one else who was so active, so successful in realizing the prophecy of Lord Caitanya.

Your mission was given to you by Lord Gaurāṅga Himself, who commanded that anyone who takes birth in the land of Bhārata-varṣa is responsible for spreading this *saṅkirtana* movement to the rest of the world. You came just to fulfill that prophecy as foretold by Bhaktivinoda Thākura.

We, as your disciples, who are most fallen and who have nothing to offer you in return, have the opportunity to assist you in this mission to save the world. This is your causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because most of us have very little knowledge of the *Vedas,* what to speak of austerity. Because of our low nature we are unfaithful to our vows, and even after initiation so many of us refuse to surrender to your lotus feet. But you are the epitome of complete patience. You never turn us away, despite our countless offenses.

Who can understand the wonderful characteristics of a pure devotee? When we, your disciples, think about you; we are simply amazed; our hearts fill with respect and affection for you. Please let us never consider you to be ordinary .Any gentleman will readily accept that you are Lord Kṛṣṇa's most confidential servant. This is confirmed in the *Bhagavad-gitā* by Kṛṣṇa Himself: "For one who explains this supreme secret to the devotees, devotional service is guaranteed, and at the end he will come to Me. There is no servant in this world more dear to Me than he, nor will there ever be one more dear." (*Bg*. 18.68-69)

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us to rid ourselves of mundane ambitions and by your causeless mercy become purified by remaining under the shelter of your instructions. You have kindly accepted us as your disciples; therefore, our only business now is to please you. To please the spiritual master means to receive his blessings. Without the blessings of the spiritual master no one can make spiritual advancement.

Therefore, we are praying to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to give us the strength and the heart to carry out your mission of spreading this Kṛṣṇa consciousness around the world.

*Your servants of Denver temple.*

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you've come to the West  
On His Divine Grace’s order to pull us out of this mess.

Satisfied in Vṛndāvana, taking shelter of the holy name,  
You set out with no other, determined to make Kṛṣṇa’s fame

In a society of negation, devoid of spiritual notions.  
Your every act or word aroused our deepest emotions.

Exposing the cheaters, never caring for their disguises,  
You proved that to serve Kṛṣṇa is meant for the wisest.

Beginning in New York, the most difficult to tackle,  
Your determination in Tompkins just a sample.

Preaching to so many hippies with distorted vision,  
You enthused them to work for the real mission.

The so-called learned, who are at a loss,  
You've scientifically converted, disproving their chaos.

Oh, if only we could maintain your pace,  
Then our service would not be such a disgrace.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us that higher taste  
So that we, too, will be able to kick on māyā’s face.

Serving your master without profit, distinction or adoration-  
By following your example we'll experience cooperation.

This is the key to advancement in life;  
To keep you at the forefront means no more strife.

Although we weren't able to appreciate your sanga,  
We humbly want to please you, by making big your bṛhad-mṛdaṅga.

Sometimes we're bewildered, not recognizing māyā’s looks,  
But you've said. "Whatever you need is in my books. "

Someday the world will behold your predictions  
How the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is the cure for all conditions.

Then they’ll appreciate how you preached without reservation  
That Kṛṣṇa’s the only relief from this material desperation.

Isn’t it wonderful how you looked upon us so mercifully?  
Your coming and going was done so gracefully.

We know we’re unqualified and don't deserve any consideration.  
But we're praying, dear Gurudeva. " Please again give us your association.”

*Your servants,*

## Dominican Republic

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Here I sit, trying to think what can I say to a man who knows everything. Its impossible to think what to speak or express to repay you for the sacrifice and compassion you had for me, who was with māyā obsessed.

With unlimited patience and spiritual pride you restructured my sense, mind, intelligence and life.

One, and only one way can I pay you for this, and that's to pick up a conditioned soul and give him what you've given me.

So I pray for your mercy and all your compassion. Give me the knowledge and strength that I may serve those who serve thee in training your grandchildren-to-be.

*Your servants,*

## East Africa

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā*  
*dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ*  
*karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.”** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.22)**

The struggle for life in this world full of strife  
Is fraught with pain and frustration.  
Our hopes, plans and dreams turn to ashes, it seems,  
As time destroys all situations.

With only the name, to the East Side you came,  
A wretched and cold, dismal place,  
To dispel the illusion and voidist confusion  
And Kṛṣṇa-sevā to replace.

With your message sublime and compassion divine,  
Indeed a true Vaikuṇṭha man,  
Imbued with devotion as deep as the ocean,  
For the cause of bhakti you stand.

You came to destroy our desire to "enjoy"  
And save us from Hell’s devastation.  
Oh! Glorious indeed are those souls who are freed  
From profit, distinction and adoration.

"Wake up, sleeping souls!" Gaurāṅga now calls.  
"Sleep not in the witch they call māyā.  
Just chant the Lord’s name and distribute His fame  
And relish the taste which is higher."

You put rascals and fools in palaces of jewels  
And gave them the wisdom of sages.  
A pure life to lead, no more anger nor greed,  
A process for men in all ages.

The scientists we face, with your words as our mace,  
And your books as divine ammunition.  
The demons, they hide as this movement spreads wide  
And establishes God's absolute position.

We all look in wonder as your bṛhod-mṛdṅga  
Gives mercy without discrimination.  
Oh! Jīvas. why roam? Vṛndāvana’s your home.  
Just chant and avoid transmigration.

There was no hope in sight for Africa s plight.  
Only ignorance. poverty and destruction.  
But with only a few. by the grace of Mahāprabhu  
And faith in your spotless instructions.

Now kīrtana and prasādam are changing their hearts.  
Your books we give free to the masses.  
Wherever we go. "Hare Kṛṣṇa” instead of "Jambo!"  
Lights up the dark. Solemn faces.

Please help us, we pray, ever slipping away.  
Falling victim to māyās enticements.  
Please give us the vision to push on your mission.  
'Midst Kalis corruption and violence.

Our faults they are great. full of pride, lust and hate,  
But your pure example’s our goal.  
Please root out these weeds of material disease  
That keep us from knowing the soul.

By your mercy the dumb can speak in all tongues.  
By your mercy the blind man can see.  
By your mercy the lame can walk once again.

Oh! Unlimited glories to thee!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our eternal Lord and master; we are begging to always remain under the shade of your lotus feet and to follow you wherever you go.

*Your fallen servants of ISKCON East Africa,*

**Nairobi**

## Eastern Europe

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet, which give shelter to so many people in this world. In the Ś*rimad-Bhāgavatam* (3.7.20) it is said:

*durāpa hy alpa-tapasaḥ*  
*Sevā Vaikuṇṭha-vartmasu*  
*Yatropagīyate nityaṁ*  
*deva-devo janārdanaḥ*

**"Persons whose austerity is meager can hardly obtain the service of the pure devotees, who are progressing on the path back to the kingdom of Godhead, the Vaikuṇṭhas. Pure devotees engage one hundred percent in glorifying the Supreme Lord, who is the Lord of the demigods and the controller of all living entities."**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the purest of the pure devotees. During your earthly presence (oh, how sweet it was!) there was not one second when you did not think of Kṛṣṇa. And not just thinking, but glorifying Him with love. Your love of Kṛṣṇa was so prominent, magnificent and radiant that just by seeing you we became convinced of your transcendental position. Due to your transcendental vision you saw everything and everybody as Kṛṣṇa’s property and as His part and parcel. Although you came to this world straight from Kṛṣṇaloka, because you were constantly engaged one hundred percent in glorifying Kṛṣṇa, you actually never left the spiritual world. Unless one dedicates himself to the service of such a pure devotee, one's life has no value. Your service consists of surrendering our bodies, our minds, our speech and our very life to the mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. This is your innermost desire, and anyone who claims to be your disciple will be glad to be in this ISKCON movement (which is nondifferent from your body), and he will try his best to please you in this regard. But alas, *māyā* is so powerful, she does everything to trap the conditioned soul, especially when he is about to get free from her clutches. So many temptations, so many desires other than serving your mission! Rejecting the offers made by *māyā.* putting aside all selfish desires and meeting all the tribulations that face a devotee busy to preach and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness-this is certainly great *tapaḥ.* We learn from the above- quoted verse that this kind of austerity pleases you the most. On the other hand, if someone is not willing to undergo such *tapaḥ* then whatever so-called austerity he might perform, that can be classified only as *alpa-tapaḥ.* or meager austerity, not enough to enable him to get your full blessings and mercy.

Therefore, our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray and hope that we will be eternally engaged in service at your lotus feet by partaking in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness to every town and village. Crawling for your blessings,

Your *spiritual children and grandchildren.*

## Ecuador

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. On this auspicious day of your appearance we would like to thank you for permitting us to stand in line with all the other devotees to offer our homage to you. Though glorifying you is like showing with a candle the brilliance of the sun, we know that our words will not go in vain, because you have descended from Kṛṣṇa-loka just to recover those nobody else could have recovered, to bring them back to Kṛṣṇa. The swan can extract the milk from a mixture of water and milk, but we will not find a swan in the dirty places. Therefore it was quite unexpected that a swanlike pure devotee like yourself would come to the most abominable part of the world to extract some desperate souls from this filthy mess and give them love of Kṛṣṇa. If we are allowed to stay under the shelter of the shade of your lotus feet, by your causeless mercy only, we want to promise in return that we will not stop distributing your books or living the simple life-style you taught us to live in the early ISKCON days. We feel separation from those days when you personally pushed us to go out to preach and not worry about our conveniences.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, without you as the center of our lives, this body is not worth being maintained. The years pass by, and still we cannot accomplish anything in comparison to your bold preaching. Please let our hearts become pure so that our preaching can become effective, and we can bring new devotees to offer their *daṇdavats* to your lotus feet. Then our hearts will feel happiness, and we can see within our minds your smiling face, your ever-smiling face. There is nothing else we want except to please you.

Remaining here, we can only cry, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We are yours; please don't leave us behind.

*Your servants from Ecuador.*

## Fiji

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Within the jurisdiction of this material world there are millions of universes. Within each of these universes millions of planets are existing. One such planet, named earth, has many oceans, of which the Pacific Ocean is one. Within this ocean lie hundreds of islands. A very group of these islands is called Fiji.

Fiji is so insignificant, yet how fortunate we are, not just to have heard about Your Divine Grace, but to have had your personal presence in Fiji a couple of times; to have a temple here whose foundation stone you laid; to have a full supply of all your transcendental books at all times; and to be a part of your ISKCON body engaged in spreading Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement to suffering humanity.

In the beginning stages of devotional life, due to our neophyte condition, we begin to think that we are rendering some significant service to Kṛṣṇa. But when reminded of your incomparable enthusiasm, perseverance and devotion to Kṛṣṇa and *guru.* and your benediction to the whole world, we are induced and inspired to constantly increase our services in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

By your personal example you have practically demonstrated that devotional service is not conditioned by material circumstances like old age (your service to Kṛṣṇa was never deterred by your advanced age), time (you established a Kṛṣṇa conscious society in this most degraded age of Kali) or place (you introduced Kṛṣṇa consciousness in America, where no spiritual culture was existing and where the four principal sinful activities were at a peak). Therefore, this gives one no reason at all to justify his statements of incapability in service to Kṛṣṇa by mentioning some material circumstances as an excuse.

Your unlimited mercy and your resolute determination to spread Lord Caitanya's name to every town and village brought you to Fiji, even though it remains such a small preaching field in comparison to the entire world, which you can easily deliver .

Before departing to bestow your unlimited mercy on fallen souls elsewhere in the universe, you have so mercifully left us under the guidance of your dear representative, Śrila Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami, whose personal care for our advancement makes us feel your transcendental presence. On this glorious Vyāsa-pūjā day of yours we pray for your blessings that we may always work for your satisfaction by satisfying your representative, and that we may never leave your service under any circumstance.

*Your insignificant servants in Fiji.*
