## France

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. On this auspicious day of your appearance we are all absorbed in remembering your divine pastimes with us. Remembering Your Grace gives us the strength and enthusiasm to carry out your eternal instructions and thus reveal to everyone your vision of a worldwide society of devotees. As your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement expands each year, all of us within this great framework of your mercy feel your presence more and more. Thus in presenting this wonderful process to the world we are, in essence, presenting you. Just as many of us were able to cut the hard knot of material attachment by a few moments of your association, so you continue to touch the hearts of so many conditioned souls by your continuing presence within the *saṅkīrtana* movement. Although there may often be difficulties in preaching your mission, we carry this burden cheerfully, knowing that each step brings us closer to you. With firm faith in your vision, we fearlessly face the greatest opposition.

Please bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we will always remain in your association by carrying your orders to perfection. You have entrusted us with your own compassion to see that each and every person may receive your blessings as we have. May we soon present to you that greatest victory. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your humble servants and friends of France Yātrā.*

## Fullerton

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We sit here contemplating how to glorify you,  
Searching for meaningful words filled with the respect that you are due.  
Vinoda-pātram, which means "joker, a laughingstock, " is what we are,  
If we dare to think that words exist which, compared to you, are on a par.

You left Vrndāvana-dhāma, although everyone thought that you should stay.  
But as Lord Caitanya's right-hand man you had a lot you had to say.  
"Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa. What is the difficulty? "you preached throughout all lands,  
And now throughout the world souls bow to you with folded hands.

You delivered the highest knowledge and showed us how to purely live.  
You woke us up from māyā's lap and taught us how to truly give.  
You freed us from attachment to all the temporary, worldly things  
And told us many stories of elevated saintly kings.

You revealed to us the purpose of this human form of life.  
You taught us how to sing and dance and freed us from all strife.  
You showed us how to cook in ghee and told us when to sleep.  
You built temples to glorify the Lord and gave us vows to keep.

The list goes on and on of all the service that you did.  
And yet for the most part your personal glories remain hid.  
For the world knows us as the Hare Kṛṣṇas, not as the Prabhupadās,  
Which is a tribute to you, a true guru and pure servant of God.

As our loving spiritual father, you've supplied us with all our needs,  
Like Vedic texts, Kṛṣṇa-prasādam and tulasī japa beads.  
Now in return let us try to see what we can do for you.  
After all, love means the reciprocation of feelings between two.

But self-realized, pure devotees like you, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Who descended to this "cosmic slum " to deliver love of God,  
Can be satisfied only by your own activities.  
Yet with our debt unpaid, we try to serve, hoping you will be pleased.

*Your servants at New Śāntipura-dhāma,*

Haripāda dāsa / Janārdana dāsa / Bhakta Jun / Phalini-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Bhakti */* Chimau Kotecha and family / Pranjivan Popat and family / Ram Singhania and family

## Gainesville

Dear *Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!

It is a well-known story how, when you visited Gainesville in 1971, you described it as a "remote corner of the world," because it is so far from the birthplace of Lord Caitanya. Although Florida is one of the four most internationally known states in the most affluent and prestigious country in the world, and although the university here is one of the largest and most important in this region, with your transcendental Kṛṣṇa conscious vision you called this place "remote." How wonderful this is! How ecstatically wonderful!

And remote it is yet. Though we've been preaching here all these years, Plaza where you spoke is still strewn with cerebroplegic bodies, stunned and spiritually devastated by *māyā s* unceasing bombardment of licentious advertising and biochemical indoctrination. Their distance from Kṛṣṇa is so great. As a person in a coma may have all material aid still not awaken, similarly these poor victims of *māyā* are being nursed by us daily, as you have taught us, but still they do not awaken.

*Some* do, of course. Many devotees have come from this campus. In fact, some of the strongest and steadiest in Florida, as well as some who have moved to other centers. And many of the students are being purified gradually, *appreciating prasādam* and the association of devotees. We continue on; you said that our *prasādam* distribution here should never stop.

Nevertheless, in general their distance remains very great-remote, as say. How to shorten that distance: this is our meditation. How to bring them close to Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu? They are so reluctant to come the distance (as are we ourselves at times). We want to bring them near .

*You* brought *us* near, so you can bring them, also. We pray to you to please do so. Please bring them near, and reveal to us how we can help. When Mahābuddhi asked about preaching to professors, you quoted *dadāmi buddhi-yagaṁ tam:* "I give the intelligence. .." But you are the *jagad-guru.* Kṛṣṇa’s direct representative, so you can do the same.) We are not so much different than they; only yesterday we were as they are now. Yet somehow or other, by your mercy we have become fortunate. (You wrote, "The young boys and girls of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement have given up the spirit of material enjoyment not because of practice but by the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and His servants.") So by your mercy we have taken the perfect process of *harināma,* and our lives are gradually becoming perfect also. In the meantime, however, we feel somewhat.bumbling, groping through our own mental darkness to assist you in your mission.

There are multifarious aspects of your mission, of course, but we in Gainesville have been charged with the particular responsibility of purifying the college campus and reclaiming the so-called students to assist this great movement that you began and are maintaining. Many times you have emphasized the importance of college preaching, saying that the colleges are our best field. Certainly many of our biggest leaders have come from college programs. So we wish to dedicate ourselves to this proposition: that the college campuses offer the best potential for an intellectual, cultural and spiritual revolution, as they bring together in a concentrated area many of the most materially intelligent people in society at a time of their lives when they are relatively open to new ideas. We are humbly requesting that you grant us your mercy so that we may actually accomplish something substantial in what seems to be one of the most favorable preaching fields in the world. We pray that this place and its poor inhabitants, by their progressive adoption of Vedic principles, may be blessed to be considered less remote.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants,*

Mahādyuti dāsa / Trilokeśvara dāsa / Jaya Jagadīśa dāsa / Raghunātha Purī dāsa / Manuśreṣṭha dāsa / Mīna-avatāra dāsa / Bhakta Udaya / Bhakta Rajan : Bhakta Tom / Nanda-patni-devī dāsi / Vrajavanesvari-devī dasī. Narottama

## Gauhati

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Another year has come, and we feel that this means to us that you have allowed us another year in your divine association. Although the sweet remembrance of your beautiful form is there in our mind, we still feel intense separation from your lotus feet.

On your morning walks you so boldly preached the message of Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and by your smiling glance you would awaken love of Godhead in the hearts of all living entities. That look of divine love you would radiate through every action of yours was the very stuff which your disciples lived on.

I remember once in Madras, when we went to meet you at the airport, as you entered the terminal the look that you showered on your devotees choked everyone and brought tears to our eyes, making us speechless, unable to move.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please keep us always in remembrance of you, and never allow us to stray from your instructions. All glories to you who have changed our sinful lives and shown us the path of devotional service. Hold our hand tight, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and never allow us to leave.

*Begging to always remain your eternal servants,*

Bhavabhuti dāsa / Śrī Jīva dāsa / Vaikuṇṭha dāsa / Ananta Purī dāsa / Sāmba dāsa / Kāmākhya dāsa / Patita-taraṇa dāsa / Kṛṣṇalāla / Bhakta Gaurav / Bhakta Bhuban / Maṅgalā gopi-devī dāsī / Ulūpī-devī dāsī / Rukmiṇi

## Ghana

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances.

We have been receiving letters from the *karmis* addressed to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda since we opened this preaching center. If *karmīs* can see that you are here with us, how we *bhaktas* should be seeing you.

He who tears asunder atheistic mentality.

He who sacrificed *samādhi* in order to serve.

When our longing to see Kṛṣṇa intensifies, then you introduce yourself.

When our longing to see you maximizes, then Kṛṣṇa manifests Himself.

What other proof could be more viable than this-that you represent Kṛṣṇa?

Remembering you is the qualification for receiving real rendition to drama of life.

Remembering you brings remedy to the *yuga* relapse.

Remembering you generates preaching propensity.

Preaching means distributing your books.

Our only sincere prayer is therefore how we can get purity, because purity is the force needed for distributing your books.

Because you mean to help us to the end, you have given us purity personified, in the name of Śrila Kīrtanānanda Swami. By his instructions we can be purified.

Because we are unworthy, we are very slow, dull, sluggish, in fact, insensitive to his instructions. That is our lamentation. Poor souls, we have no good qualities. However, we believe there are many ways you can answer this, our prayerlike question: How can we be completely sold out to your instruction, just as you were sold out to the instructions from Bhaktisiddhānta Mahārāja?

On this memorable day when you appeared in the world, we can only say, Please keep on coming.

The devotees in Takoradi temple are crying for you.

*Your fallen servants.*

## Gitā-nāgari

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Your stunning example of staunch, undeviated, unmotivated devotional service is the guiding star of my life. Every day I enact a symbolic battle of Kuruksetra right here at Gītā-nāgarī. I am in the unsurrendered position, and only by remembering your perfect example and hearing your explanations of Arjuna's plight and your purports to Kṛṣṇa's instructions is it possible to daily keep up this battle and emerge victorious, doing what Kṛṣṇa wants.

You were duty bound. You walked through the fire of ordeal to carry out the order of Kṛṣṇa and at every moment maintained your courage and humility. I am the opposite. My tendency is to be weak-hearted and always faltering and doubting Kṛṣṇa orders. But with your perfect example before me I can see the right path. I can see the right application of the devotional principles to time and circumstances. I can see all the twenty-six qualities of the pure devotee fully manifested in you, which leaves me no slack for complacency. You came down to our level and lived with us, so I can't say, "This is not for me. This is too pure for an ordinary person like me." You showed us that Arjuna is every man and surrendering process is everyone's battle of Kurukṣetra, not just that of a select few. You made Lord Caitanya's message live, so that anyone in any position of life can take to pure devotional service. It won't work for sit back in the forest and eulogize you. I have to get up off my laurels and run after you, following in your footprints, trying to catch your lotus feet. I must give up all desire for personal profit and just fill up my life with Kṛṣṇa’s desires, just like Arjuna did.

When I work, I have to eat to get energy. So when I work to preach and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness, I realize that my food is your association. Every drop of my strength and energy comes from hearing you speak, meditating on your life, your activities and your words. Your guidance, your encouragement, your blessings are the only nourishment for a conditioned soul trying to get free. Please empower me to do my part in your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in a way that is pleasing to you.

*Your servants at Gīta-Nāgarī,*

## Greece

*Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, Jagad-guru,*

With great care and attention we bow down to your lotus feet-the abode of pure devotional service-and we offer you our most humble obeisances. On this auspicious day of your appearance on the planet, we, your disciples and granddisciples that are preaching in the Greek *yātrā,* also desire to worship you, glorify you, offer you prayers and in this way keep you satisfied. Śrīla Narottama dāsa Thākura has sung, "You who have given us the gift of transcendental vision are our lord, birth after birth. By your mercy divine knowledge is revealed within the heart, bestowing *prema-bhakti* and destroying ignorance. The Vedic scriptures sing of your character." We were wandering aimlessly in the cycle of *saṁsāra,* servants of illusion, being constantly pierced by the threefold miseries of Durgā-devī for millions and millions of years. Then you came with your books, your devotees, your temples, your pure and valuable association freely distributed to the most fallen.

"We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being."

Year after year, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we realize more and more the mercy you have given us. Where would we be now if not for you and your ISKCON? We were proud and ignorant, but your pure sound opened our eyes and changed our hearts.

Here in Greece, *Guru*deva, another miracle is happening due to your mercy: uncivilized human beings get to take *prasādam,* read your books, chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and become *bhaktas.* Watching them reciting verses from Ś*rimad-Bhāgavatam,* speak nice Vaiṣṇava philosophy and behave properly, we can't but think of how you must be pleased, seeing this movement expanding all over the world.

We are confident that if Socrates or Sophocles were present, they would have surely joined us!

When you were in the middle of the Atlantic Ocean, on the *Jaladuta,* coming to the West, you wrote in your diary: " As the vast mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu conquers all directions, a flood of transcendental ecstasy will certainly cover the land. When all the sinful, miserable living entities become happy, the Vaiṣṇava's desire is then fulfilled."

We worship that desire of yours, Śrīla Prabhupāda; we make it our life and soul and our meditation, because this is our connection with you, our devotional service.

You wrote once to Śrīla Satsvarūpa in 1976, "Our first business is this book distribution. If this book distribution is managed properly, pushed on with great enthusiasm and determination, and at the same time if our men keep spiritually strong, then the whole world will become Kṛṣṇa conscious."

Let us cooperate, therefore, all together in the matter of satisfying this desire of yours, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and please continue giving us your mercy, because we are nothing without you.

*Your most fallen and insignificant servants,*

Ayodhyāpati dāsa / Ātmānanda dāsa / Citraka dāsa / Nandagopa dāsa / Dvārakānātha dāsa / Bhakta Yannis / Bhakta Yorgos / Ātmarati-devī dāsi / Bhakti-bhāva-devī dāsī / Lalitāpriyā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Popi

## Guyana

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

As confirmed by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself, we are especially fortunate to be engaged in preaching on your behalf within this lSKCON movement.

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

**“Out of many millions of wandering living entities, one who is very fortunate gets the opportunity to associate with a bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa."**

However, because of our material attachment, we are still hankering after the achievement of profit, fame and adoration in this short life span, and therefore we have a tendency to sometimes forget our great fortune. In your purports to Ś*rimad Bhāgavatam* you have written, "The contamination in the heart of a conditioned soul is like a huge accumulation of garbage created by three modes of material nature, especially the modes of *rajas* (passion) t*amas* (ignorance)." So what hope is there for us to become purified other than by taking complete shelter of your lotus feet? Śrila Bhaktivinoda Thākura has nicely described our position and our relationship with you when he sings, *anādi karama phale padi' bhavārṇava-jale, tarībāre nā dekhi upāya:* "My Lord, I do not know when I commenced material life, but I can certainly experience that I have fallen in the deep ocean of nescience. Now 1 can see that there is no way to get out of it than to take shelter of Your lotus feet."

Even though we are now bereft of your physical presence on this planet, we can still have the full advantage of the shelter of your lotus feet keeping ourselves connected to you within your ISKCON movement, which has been so expertly designed by you so as to give shelter to all conditioned souls for thousands of years to come. We must continuously pray to you for your causeless mercy and blessings so that we may become serious about expanding this mission of yours by unflinchingly carrying out the orders of your bona fide representatives and remaining always as the servants of your servants,

*Your disciples and granddisciples in Guyana,*

**New Godruma**

## Harare

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ*  
*paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande*  
*paramānanda-mādhavam*  
*Madhya 17.80*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, the deliverer the fallen souls. His mercy turns the dumb into eloquent speakers and enables the lame to cross mountains.**

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your wonderful compassion upon us fallen souls. I am writing these poor lines on behalf of all the devotees here in your recently opened temple in Harare.

As we are now gathered to celebrate your very auspicious appearance day, I must admit that I am completely unqualified to speak any words of praise befitting you: I feel just like a dwarf trying to catch the moon. The highest proof of your supernatural qualities is that I, the most wretched *jīva* soul, am somehow or other engaged in the preaching mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, only by your mercy.

And by your transcendental mercy we have come to a new country in Africa, Zimbabwe, to establish a temple in its capital, Harare. As you wrote in the Ś*rī Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* even if one chants both the syllables of the holy name of the Lord, be it in separate words and even unknowingly, the chanter still receives transcendental benefit. So people here were already chanting the holy name of Hare when we came; Hare is a call upon Śrī Kṛṣṇa to engage us in His devotional service, and therefore I understand that Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī has responded to the unconscious call of the conditioned souls here by sending devotees and establishing a Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa temple: the Harare Kṛṣṇa temple.

And Śrimatī Rādhārāṇī , who is Bhakti-devī, is enacting Her pastimes through you, who are Her very dear servant. Therefore it is ultimately you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to whom Zimbabweans owe the extraordinary boon of having Kṛṣṇa consciousness in their country; and soon another preaching center will be opened. So let this be our humble offering at your lotus feet. May it give you some transcendental pleasure, by seeing how Zimbabweans are becoming devotees and your books are being distributed in yet another African country .

As far as ourselves are concerned, we are just praying for the benediction of continuing being engaged in your service with more enthusiasm and less material motivations day after day, year after year, life after life. Please always keep us in a tiny corner of your unlimited heart, no matter how remote, and give us the strength and courage to push on Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement:

*Your eternal servants,*

Jalākāra dāsa / Devarṣi dāsa / Ekendra dāsa / Raivata Muni dāsa / Īśvara dāsa / Bhakta Louis / Bhakta Major / Vraja-līlā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Naomi / Bhaktin Santi / Shree Nagindas Vishram and family / Shree Daya Lalabhai and family / Shree Chandau Trivedy and family / Mr. Vinu Patel

## Hawaii

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Hawaii as a place you came  
And spent much of your time,  
To enliven your disciples  
And to make their lives sublime

Unlike all the conditioned souls  
Who flock here for the beach.  
You came to give your mercy,  
As your mission was to preach.

Translating in the early hours  
Till late into the night.  
Avidya you would dissipate  
Like dazzling spiritual light.

Your morning walks on Magic Isle  
Were times we can’t forget.  
But when we walk that same path now  
Our minds fill with regret.

Your meetings with the GBC  
And other leaders too  
You trained us all so carefully  
To guide our mission through

Your trip to the Big Island  
Was as so full of many topics.  
Especially how to run our farms.  
When they are in the tropics.

Your plan to spread the movement here  
To us you made it clear  
On every major island  
Soon a temple should appear.

Knowing Honolulu  
Was a most degraded place  
You installed Pañca-tattva  
By your kind and loving grace

The history of our actions here  
Was so full of offense,  
But still you tolerated-  
How your mercy is immense!

These islands seem so heavenly  
To us conditioned souls,  
So Prabhupāda. please let us not  
Lose sight of our real goals.

The suns shines bright. the air is warm.  
Jaladuta sails the sea.  
Please, Prabhupāda. expose the truth:  
From māyā set us free.

*Your loving disciples and granddisciples in the Hawaiian Islands*

## Hong Kong

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

In the Vedic literature it is said:

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande paramānanda-mādhavam*  
*Madhya 17.80*

By the mercy of the *guru,* even a dumb man can become the greatest orator, and even a lame man can cross mountains. " We are realizing this fact, practically. The spiritual master's unlimited mercy is the sum and substance of spiritual life. With the mercy of the spiritual master, a disciple can accomplish the most difficult and seemingly impossible task. Without the mercy of the spiritual master even the simplest task becomes impossible to perform for any lasting period of time.

We are seeing how you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are directly empowering your disciples and their disciples to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world. As you have said, ISKCON is your body, and this can be seen in every activity we are performing. Because you are the creator , maintainer and the life-giving force of ISKCON, it is dynamically expanding in size and increasing in potency. In the same way a puppeteer is manipulating wooden puppets, causing them to dance, you are guiding . ISKCON through all of those pure devotees who follow your instructions without alteration.

We simply have to meditate on your transcendental pastimes for renewed enthusiasm and spiritual strength. Your life was an example of perfect Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Every word you spoke or wrote, every instruction you gave, and every move you made was all done in pure devotion for the pleasure of Kṛṣṇa. Whether preaching in India, Europe or America, you tirelessly presented Kṛṣṇa consciousness to any audience who had even a small desire to listen. To the amazement of everyone, your fast-paced, lionlike preaching mood went on twenty-four hours a day, melting the stonelike hearts of millions of fallen, conditioned souls.

We are simply begging you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we may become completely absorbed in following your instructions strictly, without deviation. Let us become attached to rising very early in the morning and chanting sixteen rounds with great attentiveness and sincerity. Let us become attached to following the four regulative principles staunchly, and attending *maṅgala-ārati, tulasī-pūjā, guru-pūjā,* and Ś*rīmad-Bhāgavatam* class without fail. Let us take Kṛṣṇa*-prasādam* and nothing but Kṛṣṇa*-prasādam,* in the association of other devotees. You have told us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that if we strictly follow these basic programs as you have set them up, in a mood of devotion and cooperation, we will become more and more purified, and to the extent of that purity we will become empowered to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Although we have no qualifications, and we can never repay you for what you have given us, we pray that we may please you in some small way by tirelessly translating and printing all of your books in the Chinese language, distributing millions of your books in both Hong Kong and China, constantly preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness through various preaching programs, distributing large quantities of *prasādam,* and somehow or other make many more Chinese devotees who will assist us to do the same.

*We beg to remain your eternal servants,*

Giridhāri dāsa / Rāmaśaraṇa dāsa / Durāśaya dāsa / Hanumān dāsa / Devakīnandana dāsa / Ekādaśī dāsa / Nitāi-Gaurāṅga dāsa / Jitarati dāsa / Jayanta-kṛt dāsa / Yaśomatī-suta dāsa / Gaura-vāṇī dāsa / Jagad-*guru* dāsa / Bhakta Poon / Bhakta Peter Fung / Māninī-devī dāsī / Sādhana-devī dāsī / Harināma-devī dāsī / Kīrtanāmṛta-devī dāsī / Śacīdevī dasī / Bṛhadmṛdaṅga-devī dāsī */* Bhaktin Catherine / Bhaktin Winnie

## Israel

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your ISKCON society, which is saving the world by the distribution of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. After many world tours, you returned to Vṛndāvana sometime in October 1977 for a brief rest before taking your final journey to Kṛṣṇa. As usual, this stop was filled with preaching, translation, management and legal affairs, as you would never keep a moment to yourself-always giving everything to Kṛṣṇa and your devotees. Later on, nearing November, a newspaper article arrived one day plaining how your disciples had built and opened a wonderful new temple in South Africa. Though at the time you were quite tired, you couraged your attendant *sannyāsī* to read you the report. While the reort was being read, you were glowing, relishing the victory of your *saṅkīrtana* movement. Then the article mentioned that Kṛṣṇa consciousness makes the practitioner happy, healthy and holy. This part of the report particularly pleased you, and with a broad smile you requested the *sannyāsī,* "Please read that again." After two or three successive readings, you requested the *sannyāsī* to continue, and then the article mentioned that our business was to smash "bogus *yogīs."* Upon hearing this, you broke into laughter, and even though quite weak, you launched into attack of Ramakrishna and Vivekananda. After glorifying the superior activities of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, you said, "No one cares for their hospitals, anyway." By this time all the devotees were swimming in an ocean of ecstasy, as you, our glorious master, though worn and tired, relished the nectar of scoring another victory against *māyā.* Then the room became very quiet and still, and the *sannyāsī* broke silence by saying, "Śrīla Prabhupāda, your *saṅkīrtana* movement is ever-increasing ocean of ecstasy." To which you replied, "Hmm?" The *sannyāsī* replied, "Your movement is ā*nandāmbudhi-vardanam," and* you, although flat on your bed, immediately thrust your arm in the air-finger pointed straight as an arrow-and replied, " *Pararṁ vijayate śrī -kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtanam* Then as your weakened arm fell, all the devotees cried out, " Jaya Prabhupāda!"

At every phase of your life you showed us that Lord Caitanya ever- increasingly bestows His mercy to one who remains dedicated to spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. To this very day you are relentlessly following this path. From your transcendental literatures you are beating the *"bṛhad-mṛdaṅga,* and that sweet vibration is being heard in every town and village around the world. By this eternal sound you are rallying the people of the world and creating an army of preachers whose goal is to destroy the influence of *māyā.* We pray that by our keeping your instructions for preaching in the core of our hearts you will forever keep that great *mṛdaṅga* resounding. By your expertise at playing this drum we will lose all desire to hear anything else. The subtle voices of our minds, the tinkling enticeṁents of *māyā-your* delicate rhythm on the *bṛhad- mṛdaṅga* will pound away all our desires for independence, fruitive enjoyment, liberation. We will only become maddened to worship you and follow your instructions. Then as our long journey draws to a close, our sentiment will also be," *Paraṁ vijayate śrī-kṛṣṇa saṅkirtanam."*

By your mercy, you have appeared in our doomed and forlorn lives and have renovated everything. You have given us the *svārtha-gati* and the ś*reya-uttama.* You have opened our eyes to the transcendental reality of Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityananda. Once on a walk you were explaining that in order to see Kṛṣṇa we must have spiritual eyes. As one of your disciples was lamenting the fact that our eyes are only material, you replied by shouting, "Then I'll pluck out your eyes!" Jaya Prabhupāda!

You have given us gifts that bear no price. We are hoping that in return, you will allow us to become staunch guardians of your words, your movement, your books, that are all designed to save the world. Please always remain resting in our hearts, leaving no room for sense gratification. Slay the demoniac mental influence, so we will go and gather every soul in every town and village on the globe. Please continue to give us the inspiration to translate, print and distribute as many of your transcendental literatures in as many languages as we possibly can. Please help us to fill your universal house of ISKCON, and in order to accomplish all, please grant us the ability to remember you twenty-four hours a day.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are saving the world. We pray that on this auspicious day of your appearance, millions and millions of garlands of praise and glorification will be offered to you. Please glance our way and inspire us for lifetimes of service.

*Your servants at the battlefront, the Israeli Yātrā,*

Ayodhyāpati dāsa / Dānavīra dāsa / Guṇa-avatāra dāsa / Nitya-siddha dāsa / Nitya-līlā dāsa / Īśsvara-Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Bhakta Shlomo I / Bhakta Shlomo II / Devahūti-devī dāsī / Jalāṅga-devī dāsī / Mahā-Lakṣmī-devī dāsī / Rādhā-devī dāsī / Rādhā-Mādhava-devī dāsī / Bhaktāmṛta-devī dāsī / *Vaiṣṇavaī*-devī dāsī */* Bhaktin Tsili

## Italy

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances at your lotus feet. You have always made it very clear that no one is disqualified from performing devotional service, provided he is prepared to receive the mercy of the spiritual master. Ā*cāryera mata sei, sei mata sāra:* "The order of spiritual master is the active principle in spiritual life." This is seen practically by the fact that those who have taken your orders seriously are alive and growing in spiritual life, remaining safe from the onslaught of Kali-yuga, whereas others, who have not taken shelter of your order-their spiritual progress has stopped. You are so merciful, though, that as as one takes it up again he is immediately alive and safe again under protection. You have that power to bring the spiritually dead back to life, just as when the cowherd boys and cows drank the poisonous water from Kāliya they actually died, but just by glancing over them Kṛṣṇa brought them back to life. So we are realizing that just by your glance you have brought us back to life, even after our lying in a condition for uncountable lifetimes.

Recently Śrīla Satsvarūpa dāsa Goswami has written an account of the glorious expansion of the distribution of your books. By studying this account along with the excerpts from your letters and lectures in regard to book distribution, we can realize very clearly the mission to be carried on by your followers. One foolish person who had previously been a devotee told one of your devotees that, "The trouble with you ISKCON devotees is that all you care about is printing and distributing your books." This unfortunate person gave a nice definition of a true disciple-that he only cares about the order of his spiritual master and he never tries to adjust or compromise that order, but with a simple heart adjusts his whole life and takes to executing that order. We are seeing after year that those who are taking up this order in a spirit of cooperation are experiencing your association in a more and more deep as they see themselves engaged as empowered instruments in your hands for expanding your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

You have given us the only real hope in our lives in a world full of people whose lives have become hopeless due to the bogus propaganda of so-called scientists and leaders. The transcendental knowledge that your have left is the only light to defeat these rascals, who are misleading the world in such a hopeless direction filled with untold suffering. So we that you allow us to follow you eternally and serve your mission, life after life, wherever you choose to send us.

*Your eternal servants in Italy Yātrā*

## Lagos

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You appeared in this world in order to fulfill the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu-*yāre dekha, tāre kaha ‘Kṛṣṇa-upadeśa:* to spread the holy name of Kṛṣṇa to every town and village throughout the world.

Over the years, I have slowly, gradually, painfully come to understand and accept this mission. To be a disciple means to follow the instructions of the spiritual master. But to attain the full mercy, one's mind and intelligence must be fully in agreement with the will of the spiritual master.

There is little doubt, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to what your will is. It is the same as Śrī Śrī Śrimad Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī's. It is the same as Bhaktivinoda Thākura's. It is the essence of the six Gosvāmīs', and it is the same as that of Lord Caitanya Himself: "Therefore, I order every man within this universe to accept this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and distribute it everywhere." You have always wanted an army of preachers-not just eating and sleeping. Religion without philosophy is sentiment. The Christian may glorify Jesus; the devotee may glorify you. But who is actually doing the will of the spiritual master? When will that day come when I fully surrender to you?

When will the day come, O friend of the fallen, ocean of mercy, when an follow in your wonderful footsteps and preach always and everywhere? When can we become worthy disciples? This body is yours. The very life breath is yours. When will we be able to vigorously use these bodies to execute your order? Without receiving your merciful glance, O Śrīla Prabhupāda, life has no meaning. It is simply like the breathing of bellows. Real life means preaching!

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on behalf of your disciples and grand disciples here at ISKCON Lagos, may we always be engaged in your divine service to assist you in your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness to every town and village all over the world. Please be merciful upon us. Please give us the shade of your lotus feet. Your mercy is all that we are made of.

Here in Nigeria the people are mostly covered by the modes of ignorance and passion. But because they are religious by nature, they are always receptive to hearing about Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Many new devotees are joining, and our FOLK Club is increasing rapidly. Traveling preaching programs all over Nigeria are successful among both the Indian expatriates and Nigerians. Nigeria is still a wide-open preaching field, with unlimited opportunities for preaching. We are just now expanding by opening a new temple in Port Harcourt. By sticking very tightly to Your Divine Grace's instructions and personal example, we hope to see your ISKCON movement grow all over Nigeria-and the world.

*Your eternal servants,*

Upananda dāsa / Oṁkāra dāsa / Kālīdāsa dāsa / Śruti dāsa / Jagadiśa dāsa / Mahā-Viṣṇu dāsa / Triyuga dāsa / Pradyumna dāsa / Mākhana-taskara dāsa / Śrīvatsa dāsa / Arjuna dāsa / Candra dāsa / Keśava dāsa / Citraketu dāsa / Bhakta Emmanuel / Bhakta Seth / Bhakta Chinedu / Bhakta Eno / Bhakta Adisa / Bhakta Gallant / Bhakta Bassey / Bhakta Layi / Bhakta John Williams / Bhakta Bennard Orbih / Vṛndā-devī dāsī / Iśāni-devī dasī / Rādhārāṇi-devī dāsī / Gopī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Rachel / Bhaktin Cynthia

## Laguna Beach

De*ar Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

In this most auspicious day of your appearance we come before your divine *mūrti* to beg charity from you, because you are the most charitable person. In today's , society people are extremely anxious to get more and more things for sense gratification, but you have taught how to be satisfied with only what we need. Your mercy is all that we really need, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We have not come to beg from you material things for our sense gratification. That is not the business of a spiritual master. So please give us your blessings:

- that we may never deviate from the line of service you have drawn.
- that we may become ideal representatives of Your Divine Grace.
- that we may never compromise in our preaching.
- that we may once and for all give up flirting with *māyā.*
- that we may be able to tolerate any difficulties that arise in the course of preaching.
- that we may remain within the shelter of your ISKCON house, rendering real devotional service up until our last breath.
- that we may work in a cooperative manner to push on your mission under the guidance of your GBC.
- that we may be satisfied in performing all the transcendental engagements you have so kindly given us.
- that we may continue to chant the holy name without offense.

*Begging to remain your servants forever at New Jhārikhṇḍa Mandira,*

## Lake Huntington

To His Divine Grace Jagad-*guru* Śrīla Prabhupāda, on the occasion of his all-auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā. day,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Last night you came to us again in yet another wonderful way. It was Gaura-pūrṇimā eve, Mahāprabhu’s appearance day.  
Although you've given us His festival, actually you are the star,  
For by His desire, His mercy you've spread near and far.  
" Worship Gaurāṅga, " you've taught, especially on this auspicious day,  
Yet eclipsing even Him, you are gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe.  
" Always chanting, remember Kṛṣṇa, " the secret given by you,  
Yet this simple instruction we constantly fail to do.  
Then you, compassion personified, show causeless mercy  
And tell us, "It’s all right; somehow you just remember me. "  
Even this we cannot do. O Prabhupāda, help us, please.  
Then into our minds you push yourself with the greatest expertise.  
You appear through your books, the Bhāgavatam and the Gīta,  
Nectarof Instruction and Devotion, Caitanya-caritāmṛta..  
And so many more as your writings keep you ever alive.  
Yet you come in other ways, insuring we spiritually survive.  
On countless tapes your voice rings out with the message of Godhead,  
Rousing us from sleeping in māyā's lap, our eternal bed.  
On videotapes your form and sound appear to us once again,  
In your instructions. your letters, the memories told by your intimate men.  
In the Prabhupāda-līlāmṛta your authorized biography,  
The Prabhupāda-līlā, Servant of the Servant, Śrīla Satsvarūpa’s poetry,  
In your mūrtiform you appear, adorning each temple room,  
As our fledgling Prabhupāda-consciousness you so expertly groom.  
Yet even with all this, still you do not relent,  
Showing very clearly you're Kṛṣṇa's mercy for us especially sent.  
Last night you came to us again in yet another wonderful way. This Gaura-pūrṇimā eve, Mahāprabhu’s appearance day,  
Amid laughing and tears, we viewed your film biography.  
Increasing memory of you is the meaning of ever-increasing ecstasy.  
On this, your Vyāsa-pūjāi, one request-if you'll only be so kind-  
O Prabhupāda, please always keep yourself fixed in our mind.

*Your children and grandchildren at New Puruṣottama- Kṣetra, ISKCON of Lake Huntington*

## Lima

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā*  
*dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ*  
*karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.”** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.22)**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust , of your lotus feet. You are so merciful that through your disciples you arrived here in Lima to save us from hell and lead us to Kṛṣṇa instead. Not long ago we visited a big company, disguised in *karmī* clothes, and gave the principal a copy of *Science of Self-Realization.* When he saw your picture, he exclaimed: "I know this man! He travelled alone from India to SA. Alone and with no money. But he got to establish an enormous religious movement, and thousands of young people are following him. I read his books. They are really unbelievable!" Right then we almost ,exploded with pride. And that is not the only case. The world is starting to recognize you, and we want to give our lives so it will not be long until the whole world knows Your Divine Grace. Bless us to have enough strength.

We also want to thank you for having sent your beloved son Śrīla Jayapatāka Mahārāja, who is an ocean of mercy, which he bestows unlimitedly on this land. We think that the best way to glorify and satisfy you is by means of serving his lotus feet. Please never leave us without protection nor without his association.

*Your unworthy servants of Lima Mandira.*

## Long Island

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

You are the empowered representative of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, who has come to take the name of Gaurāṅga to every town and village. You are the factual *jagad-guru.* the spiritual master of the entire planet. By your translation, publication and distribution of tran- scendental literatures you have brought the name of Kṛṣṇa to every man's front door.

Your vehicle for accomplishing these feats is your ISKCON movement, which you have created to give an opportunity for people in general to associate with devotees. *Saṅkīrtana* means to congregationally or cooperatively glorify the Supreme Personality of Godhead. You demonstrated that a large, unified preaching movement can change the world. It is the duty of every disciple to carry on your mission of worldwide preaching. Following in your footsteps means to push on your ISKCON society, just as you did.

Before departing this world, you instructed us that our love for you would be demonstrated by how much we cooperate in your absence. Understanding this instruction, we must make this cooperation for preaching our life and soul. Through your eternal instructions we will never be separated from you.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please place us as atoms of dust under your lotus feet.

*Your servants at ISKCON Long Island.*

## Los Angeles

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

To the Western world you came,  
Without desire for material gain.  
The message of Kṛṣṇa you boldly expound,  
And because of this you are forever renowned.

Your purity and valor no one can replace.  
Just like your guru, you carry the mace  
To destroy impersonalist philosophy that we all embrace.  
O Jagad-guru. Śrīla Prabhupāda, help us. Your Divine Grace.

The position of guru is second to none,  
And for us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the ultimate one.  
Your teaching and preaching will never end.  
The mission of Caitanya we can never bend.

Śrī Śrī Rukmiṇi-Dvārakādhīśa to love and to serve.  
You gave us more than we will ever deserve.  
To distribute your books and prasādam is so nice,  
And by Their mercy we employ every device.

Rathayātrā, hari-nāma, restaurants and BBT:  
For us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a desire tree.  
Giving the service by which one is forever free.  
As our ever well-wisher you guarantee.

## Mauritius

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda Glorification

It was in 1896,  
A momentous year indeed,  
Caitanya's teaching first reached the West  
lant the bhakti-latā-bīja.

Certainly it was no coincidence  
that in that year you took birth;  
Kṛṣṇa personally ordered you  
To come and save the earth.

Bhaktivinoda Thākura predicted  
That a great soul would soon appear  
And deliver the Lord's holy name  
For every town and village to hear.

There is no doubt or question  
Within my heart or mind  
That the Thākura was speaking of you  
As the one who would appear in time.

You were raised by pure devotees  
And favored by Rādhārāṇī from birth  
Y'ou were always fully Kṛṣṇa conscious  
And a perfect example of how to live on earth.

The order of your Guru Mahārāja  
Remained always in your heart.  
And when the proper time arose  
To the West you did depart.

Leaving India with only forty rupees  
And without a helping hand,  
Yet you carried the real valued possession:  
Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam and Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta's command.

You traveled alone through Kali's jungles,  
Confident in Kṛṣṇa's help.  
Nothing could stop your preaching work  
Even amidst the worst of health.

Your beginnings were most humble-  
many took notice at all,  
But you knew Kṛṣṇa had a plan  
And soon success would call.

Year by year more temples rose  
Like flowers in the spring,  
And by your words you maintained them all  
Just like a saintly king.

You've given the greatest gift  
And the greatest service to humanity  
By translating the Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam  
To stop Kali-yugas sinful activities.

Your glories have no limit,  
But one shines above the rest:  
Your never deviated from the order of your guru  
And you never failed any test.

You are benediction personified,  
Showering nectar from your mercy cloud.  
Frankly speaking, I must admit,  
To be your disciple I feel quite proud.

Your life has created good fortune  
For every living entity.  
Never has such a world savior existed  
In the entire human history.

*Your servants at ISKCON Mauritius,*

## Melbourne

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your feet. These obeisances are not offered as a single act, or even as a series of acts, to indicate our submission to your divine order. They are a continuous attitude which has grown up within us. They are the fruit of your fond administrations towards us, your foolish, half-conscious servants.

The *Vedas* state the qualifications of the bona fide spiritual master following in the footsteps of Śrīla Vyāsadeva. In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* we find further confirmation of the unique qualities and attributes of the mercy incarnation of the Supreme Lord, Your Divine Grace. All these instructions and statements of śā*stra* help us to understand and appreciate your unexcelled qualifications and your nearness to Lord Kṛṣṇa. They do not make it easier for us to tolerate our separation from you.

We are not bereft of the understanding that you are undoubtedly present within our lives by your instruction and by the external manifestation of your desire, ISKCON. These are our only consolations. These absolute, transcendental facts do not obscure from us, however, the painful truth that we will never again in this lifetime anticipate with joyful relish your cherished arrival. There will be no more wildly exhilarating greetings of Your Divine Grace at the airports of this world. Never again will we walk with you through the parks and along the beaches which you have transformed into *tīrthas* by your sacred footfalls. We have not a shred of hope to again catch a treasured glimpse of you entering the temple room to offer your perfect obeisances to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, who stand in blissful appreciation of Their beloved, intimate associate. We are doomed to the lamentable fate of never being able to personally approach you in submission to inquire and render personal service. The book appears to be closed on this exciting, inspiring and supremely beneficial period of our wretched lives. But no, somehow we are unable to completely abandon hope. Sometimes we know it is all still possible. It has never really ended.

We understand from the *Bhagavad-gītā that* whatever is eternal has no cessation. The *guru* is an eternal reality. How then is it possible that there has been a cessation of your presence? It cannot have happened. Sometimes we are fortunate enough to experience confirmation of this fact in the discharge of our service. We can actually feel that you are becoming pleased. Conversely, when the waves of *māyā* begin to wash over us and threaten to engulf us in a sea of sinful illusion, it is the perception of your displeasure which sets us right again. Our love for you is a living, breathing presence which encourages and chastises according to your own divine, transcendental and sweet will. We are no less in your personal presence than ever we were. It is this secret, personal, beautiful truth which is nourishing and sustaining us. Were it not so, long past we would have returned to the night of the living dead, sleep-walking minions in the kingdom of darkness from which you have rescued us.

Our desire to praise you is never well accomplished. Our words fail to convey the depth of gratitude we hold and can never repay. Therefore we simply submit our prayer to the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, that our lives may become a monument to your greatness. It is to you that we owe all credit for whatever may be accomplished through our miserable efforts. It is you who have inspired us to act in other than our own selfish interest. It is you who are our lord and master birth after birth. It is you alone who have come to extinguish this blazing fire of material existence which seared and scarred us with the pain of innumerable material lives. It is you, our beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, for whom we shed tears of separa- tion in the secret recesses of our hearts, which are now softened due to our love for you. Whey have you gone away and left us weeping in the sorrow of our loss? But then, how can we imagine that this could be our loss? Your *Guru* Mahārāja left you in the same way, but you have shown us by your example that you never *fell* into the illusion that he had *actually* gone. He stayed *with* you *and guided* you *and personally di*rected you in your service. How can we think that you would act any differently towards us than you have learned from your *Guru* Maharaja? You have told us that if there is any credit due, it is only the fact that you have followed the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta without deviation. The order of the *guru* is eternal; thus your following continues. We are absolutely certain of this.

We see you in your instructions. We express our love for you by following those instructions. We see you in your ISKCON manifestation. We express our love for you by cooperating with and serving that body. We see you in your *mūrti* form, and we bathe and dress and worship you in that form. We also see you personally as you will remain eternally known to us. You have just appeared directly in front of us. Our eyes are fixed upon you. We attempt to comprehend your presence, to fix this moment eternally within us. Your golden form is draped in soft, elegant saffron robes. There is a chill in the early morning air, so you are wearing a bright orange sweater that has been given to you by one of the multitude of your followers. Your right hand is within your bead bag. Your left hand raises into the air just to your shoulder level, and you wave your hand to us in a benediction salute. Your face is grave, and we stand in awe of your gravity. You are softly chanting. You pause momentarily to allow your intimate disciples, my senior Godbrothers who will accompany you, to catch up. They arrive within a moment, carrying your baggage, which is surprisingly scant. The door of the car is open, and the motor is running. A continuous puff of exhaust is visible in the brisk morning air. A devotee has opened the door, and you advance toward it. Your walk is majestic, strong and purposeful. Your back is straight, your head held high. You are a lion, a saint. We simply exist to serve and please you. Oh, please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, look upon us! We are living only for that glance. You are wonderful! Your back is now toward us. You pause and turn back in our direction. We, your surrendered servants, are beaming in elation. Your grave countenance breaks into a broad, golden sparkling smile. " Just chant Hare Kṛṣṇa," you say. " Jaya, Śrīla Prabhupāda, Prabhupāda, Prabhupāda, Prabhupāda!" Your chariot disappears into the distance. We can only count the moments until we see you again.

*Your loving servants at Melbourne Mandira*

## Montreal

Śrīla Prabhupāda

O rare emblem of unalloyed devotional service,  
You are the living force  
Of our tender devotional creepers.  
Of compassionate mahātmā,  
With unseen expertise  
You have planted unlimited bhakti-latās ...  
That we strive to assist you:  
A miracle done by His Divine Grace!

O unique Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
You have drowned the seeds of impersonalism  
In tidal waves of Vaiṣṇava literatures;  
You have usurped the ruling power  
Of the material blind leaders;  
You have brought Vaikuṇṭha on earth.  
Let us worship your golden instructions  
As our token of gratitude for your mercy.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most blessed day  
We feel eternally indebted to you.  
Kindly forgive our unfortunate offenses  
And bless us with the power to preach  
The message of Lord Kṛṣṇa like you did.

*Your humble servants of ISKCON Montreal, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara Mandira,*

## Murwillambah

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

On this auspicious day, we, the residents of " New Govardhana, along with the exalted personalities found throughout the various planetary systems, are moved in remembrance of your great self. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most revered and pure sage, a character and holy personality beyond the realm of complete appreciation. However, on this auspicious day, our minds are laboring, trying to accommodate the vast infinitude of pure goodness that you embody.

You came to remind us of what we had forgotten, to remove malaise of the people and to avert the exponential growth of suffering and calamity. Even in the darkest regions where the sun was fretting fuming in a vain attempt to purify and brighten, by a mere glance you illuminated and made everything wonderful.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our eternal father and guide. Please bless us with the strength and intelligence to assist you always.

*Your eternal servants,*

## Nepal

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances.

You had asked us to make a "stronghold of consciousness in Nepal," and still the task is only just begun. There is so much preaching to be done in so many villages and cottages, over so many mountains and rivers. It seems such a daunting task.

We are just your children, and we depend on your mercy to have the strength and intelligence to be successful in our preaching mission.

You are always somehow mercifully present in your spiritual family of devotees, and we are praying, therefore, "Please don't let us fall away from the association of your beloved disciples, or else we will lose hold of your lotus feet."

Prabhupāda,  
Master at whose feet all masters sit.

Established that Kṛṣṇa is more than hearsay.

God's greatest gift to mankind.

Master at whose feet all masters sit.

You never once made any compromise.

## Newark

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine self.

Meditating upon you and the effulgent gifts emanating from the spring flowing from your love for Kṛṣṇa and your spiritual master is actual happiness. We worship you, Lord Kṛṣṇa's divine manifestation of pure spiritual existence, empowered with ever-expanding *Kṛṣṇa-prema.* By your smile or serious contemplation, by your glance or facial gesture, you awaken our dormant affection for Lord Kṛṣṇa and His devotees. Your instructions melt the iceberg of our hearts and allow Lord Kṛṣṇa's sublime pastimes to flow through our minds and immerse our souls in divine thought.

Others before you spoke of Lord Kṛṣṇa, but only your spontaneous love for Him and your spiritual master could attract us. As the key to our long-neglected treasure of love for Kṛṣṇa, only you can save us. Śrīla Prabhupāda, at every moment many souls, forced by their sinful *karma,* take bodies in lower species of life, missing their opportunity to awaken their love for Kṛṣṇa. Unfortunately, we lack the potency to save them or ourselves from the grinding wheel of material existence. But we are confident that gradually your love for us is removing our material attachments and thus enabling us to assist you in bringing Lord Kṛṣṇa’s other children back to the spiritual kingdom. Let us remain close to your lotus feet as tiny instruments in your glorious movement.

*Your servants in Newark, Delaware,*  
Prahlādānanda Swami / Dvārakādhīśa dāsa / *Kṛṣṇa*-nāma dāsa

## New Barṣāṇa Farm Community

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

We are humbly prostrating ourselves at your lotus feet, praying for the words that can adequately describe your unlimited greatness and glory. This most blessed of your holy appearance will be observed for thousands of years to come, and millions of devotees will pray to you for guidance and strength as well as repeatedly thank you for all you have given.

The title *jagad-guru* can only be given to you, for you are capable of saving the entire universe by your unlimited kindness and mercy. You showed us that your concern for every living being was more important than even your own comfort. You tirelessly travelled and preached to the far corners of the earth, while still having time to write your transcendental translations to the Ś*rimad-Bhāgavatam* and *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* and simultaneously writing to your thousands of disciples, building huge temples, starting Vedic *varṇāśrama* communities, establishing *guru-kulas.* FATE, and the list goes on and on.

The remembrance that sticks most vividly in our minds, however your firm attachment and commitment to the instructions of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmi Mahārāja. We can only hope and pray that we may somehow please you, even though we have no qualifications, for by pleasing the spiritual master, one gets the mercy of Kṛṣṇa.

Our lives are surrendered to you. Please guide us through the forest of the material world. Your expert vision is the only hope for us all.

*We beg to be always fixed at your lotus feet,*  
Your disciples and granddisciples at New Barṣāṇa

## Newcastle

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most fallen obeisances.

As a young boy I used to study maps and atlases with a view to someday visiting many exotic and far-off lands. I would remember the names of many cities countries. Now each year I anxiously await the Vyāsa-pūjā book commemorating your victorious appearance in this world . I enjoy looking at all the names, new and old, of your centers in many cities throughout almost every land on this planet. Some names I have even heard of. Such is the adventure of this *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Now, by the order of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and through your grace, we are realizing this adventure. Through your vast distribution books and your own personal travel, you have completely encircled planet, giving us the framework to start. Just like in children's colouring books, you have already traced out the Kṛṣṇa consciousness of the world, and now we have the pleasure of coloring it in. You have given us the colors. What could be more colorful than your books? The windows to the spiritual world, where all colors are perfect and complete. And as our loving father you guide our hands through the instructions contained within your books.

Sometimes, like foolish children, we try to pull away and become impatient, but deep inside we know that we can never become satisfied like that. And as we again look up at you, you smile to see us return, and again point out the beautiful picture of Kṛṣṇa.

We pray that we may surrender to you, so that you may guide us without a struggle, so that we may quickly fill in this picture of complete Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Dear Śrīla. Prabhupāda, you are our loving father, and you have given us everything.

Your foolish servants of Newcastle Yātrā, Australia

## New Delhi

**Eight Prayers to Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Our guru is receiving grace from ocean s mercy store.  
Just as the cloud does water on the forest fire pour,  
So does our guru save this world from flames of gross desire-  
His love, like rain, puts out the pain of lust, which burns like fire.  
There’s none so generous as he, a cloud of mercy sweet.  
We bow, expecting rainfall from his gracious lotus feet.

Our guru loves to sing and dance in pure ecstatic mood  
And spread Gaurāṅga's saṅkīrtana, his heart with love imbued,  
And as he turns and swirls in trance and on the name does call,  
His hair stands up. his body shakes, and tears in torrents fall.  
We pray that we may sing and dance at home and on the street  
And bring all songless souls to prance before his lotus feet.

Our guru loves to worship at the temple of the Lord,  
Where forms of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa are devotedly adored.  
He leads his dear disciples in such services divine  
As dressing up the Deities in garments fair and fine  
And cleaning out the temple; thus our lives become complete  
By guileless surrender at our guru s lotus feet.

Our guru offers Kṛṣṇa four delicious kinds of food  
(Analyzed as food that’s licked, drunk, sucked and chewed);  
And when he sees the devotees accept prasād with glee,  
His heart is glad, for such prasād fills life with jollity.  
What joy it is to take prasād! Thus, even as we eat,  
Devoid of greed, we can recall our guru’s lotus feet.

There’s but one thing to which our guru eagerly aspires,  
Of which he hears and talks and sings and never ever tires-  
Our guru longs to taste at every step the loving games  
Of Rādhikā and Mādhava, Their features, forms and names.  
Such pure and sinless subjects, so refreshing to repeat,  
Are relished by the mercy of the guru’s lotus feet.

Our guru lends his expert hand to aid the love affair  
Of Rādhikā and Mādhava, the transcendental pair.  
He helps the maids and sakhis with their confidential plans  
To unite the loving couple in the blissful rāsa dance.  
'Tis also by his expertise we fallen souls can meet,  
Uniting in the holy name and service to his feet.

The guru and the Lord are one and equal in this way:  
The guru is God’s faithful son and says what God would say.  
So all the scriptural books confirm and all the saints agree  
That God and guru be obeyed and worshiped equally.  
The guru, therefore, sits aloft upon th' exalted seat,  
And we small souls should try our best to serve his lotus feet.

'Tis by the guru’s grace alone that Kṛṣṇa is obtained;  
Without his grace, if he's displeased, no benefit is gained.  
So we should e'er remember him and glorify his name  
And try throughout the universe to propagate his fame.  
Three times a day we bow to him and humbly do entreat:  
"O Prabhupāda, please grant us shade beneath your lotus feet!"

*Your eternal servants,*

## New Gauḍadeśa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

As I sit here in my room thinking about what to write as a suitable homage to offer to you, on this, the most holy of days, the day when we celebrate your appearance into this world, a little phrase which you composed has come to mind.

Books are the basis.  
Preaching is the essence.  
Purity is the force.  
Utility is the principle.

In this short verse the essence of your instructions to your disciples is given, that we can successfully carryon what you have spent a whole lifetime building up.

Also, as we take a look at your life we can see that you are perfectly showing the example of how all these things can be manifested by one who takes very seriously the instructions of his *Guru* Mahārāja.

Many times in the past we would hear through the BBT newsletter or from some travelling devotee how you would delight in reports of your books being distributed on a large scale, and how they were being given a good reception by the people at large.

In fact, you once said that when you heard of the endeavors of the book distributors then this was giving you new life.

You spent your every energy in preaching to the suffering souls of this world, and you did not spare a single thought for your own comforts.

Lord Kṛṣṇa states in *Bhagavad-gitā* that there is none so dear to Him as one who preaches His glories. How dear a servant to Him are you, who gave up everything to this preaching mood!

*Pavitram* means pure, and what better word to use in describing the state of your heart. Just as a spotless mirror is able to give a true reflection of the person who stands in front of it, so by the strength of your pure heart we are gradually able to see the Supreme Lord shining out from the pages of Ś*rīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gitā.*

Many persons have spoken on the pastimes of the Lord, but because they were not pure there was no effect. .

But you have set ablaze our stonelike hearts with your descriptions of Kṛṣṇalīlā.

You are always expert in utilizing all things in the service of the Lord. The Māyāvādīs, out of fear of falldown, artificially renounce the things in this world. But you have shown us how to use everything in Kṛṣṇa's service. You are never afraid to act, because you know who is the real proprietor and in whose service all things should be used.

You are beyond time and space, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and even before your birth you were eternally Kṛṣṇa conscious. You came and showed us how to serve Kṛṣṇa; how can we ever repay you?

We are feeling very small and weak in the face of this gigantic task you have left us, and we are simply begging for your mercy that we may be able to give to others what you have given to us.

*Your eternal loving disciples and granddisciples from ISKCON New Gauḍadeśa,*

Uśāpati dāsa / Krodheśvara dāsa / Śrīvatsa dāsa / Haribhaktivilāsa dāsa / Devaprastha dāsa / Yudhiṣṭhira dāsa / Kamalākanta dāsa / Pāṇḍu-vijaya dāsa / Viṣṇugadā dāsa / Surānanda dāsa / Maha-puruśa dāsa / Bhakta Noel / Līlāvatī-devī dāsī / Caraṇāmṛta-devī dāsī / Manoharā-devī dāsī / Nandāpāda-devī dāsī / Sitā / Govinda / Padmalocana / Rasika / Japamālā / Jāmbavatī */* Bhaktin Charlie

## New Jersey

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Lord Kṛṣṇa assures us that He appreciates even a small service done with sincerity. Unfortunately, this statement disqualifies us. But we have heard that even low-class persons can progress by taking shelter of the Lord's pure devotee, and that no such service ever goes in vain. These truths are our only hope and solace. You manifest Lord Kṛṣṇa's mercy. Please be merciful and accept our service. Please glorify your movement by blessing even us with the ability to spread the holy name within ourselves and to every town and village in this part of the world.

*Your servants at ISKCON New Jersey (Towaco),*

## New Kurukṣetra

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our menial obeisances at your lotus feet. You once told your disciple Śrīla Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami that in the world it is considered to be a higher platform when one is lazy-intelligent rather than being active-intelligent or active-unintelligent. So, your disciple Śrīla Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami was then eager to become lazy-intelligent for you. But you then told him that you wanted your disciples and followers to become active-intelligent, because there is so much to do in this young, worldwide movement. How many times have we heard, "Work *now-samādhi* later',?

Although we are not at all qualified on our own, we are being sustained in whatever devotional service we perform solely by the nectarean juice of your transcendental desire. Your causeless mercy has brought Kṛṣṇa to our lives, and it is continuing to give us our very means of livelihood-loving devotional service unto the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

So now, more than ever, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because we have matured another year's worth in our realizing the immensity of your selfless contribution to the world, and because we are all still young and healthy in body and mind, and we are eager to work intelligently to please our lord and master, we here at New Kurukṣetra have to fall down onto the ground with palms folded and pray to you, that our very life depends on it: "Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we love you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Let us do something wonderful to glorify you, to let the world know of the greatest of all heroes."

Please allow this to be our offering.

*Your servants at New Kurukṣetra. Oklahoma farm,*

Paśupati dāsa / Divyajñānānanda dāsa / Mathurā-pati dāsa / Yādavācārya dāsa / Gopāla dāsa / Dāmodara dāsa / Garuḍa dāsa / Guru-Gaurāṅga dāsa / Jaya Gopāla dāsa / Raṇacora dāsa / Śyāmasundara dāsa / Śukānanda dāsa / Yaśomatīnandana dāsa / Bhakta Mike / Bhakta Rob. Bhaktin Liz / Bhakta Jim. Bhaktin Kathy. Rādhārāṇi. Rāsavihārī

## New Orleans

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Not very long ago, our conception of religion was that God had come to suffer for our sins, and we should confess that we are sinners, put it all on God, be forgiven, and then go on with our schemes for enjoying this body, making God our order-supplier.

Then you came, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and dispelled our impersonalism and voidism. You gave us knowledge of who God is, how to stop sinning, and that our body was meant for serving God; performing t*apasya* would bring real enjoyment. You were executing the order of your *Guru* Mahārāja and fulfilling the mission of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. You were creating a transcendental ISKCON family, and you were allowing the most fallen to become your sons and daughters.

You told us, "Go in like a needle, come out like a plow." Here in New Orleans, we are steadily piercing *māyā:s* bubble, confident that one day your glories will rightfully be proclaimed. When you visited us, you said we should open a restaurant, and now we have fulfilled that desire. We are also trying to build you a mansion following in the footsteps of your Kīrtanānanda Swami, who has built your palace and set the standard to worship you. You have sent us your Rūpānuga Prabhu and Jayapatāka Swami as our co-GBC to guide our service to you. Your Satsvarūpa Goswami has kept us all in ecstasy by describing your pastimes. Your Hṛdayānanda Goswami has wonderfully carried on your great work of Ś*rīmad-Bhāgavatam.*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us your mercy in so many ways, and you are empowering your senior disciples to continue that mercy. You are everywhere, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are ISKCON. Please allow us to endeavor to execute your every desire.

We were all thinking,  
"Everything is mine. "  
Then Prabhupāda came,  
spreading mercy divine.

We were looking for jobs-  
"Do you have a degree?"  
Then Prabhupāda said,  
"Come chant Hare Kṛṣṇa  
take some prasādam;  
That will please me.”

We were low-class and dirty,  
Wanting wine, women and song.  
Then you had to show us  
What was right and what was wrong.

We were all confused,  
Full of indecision.  
Then you gave us a goal:  
Spread Lord Caitanya’s mission

We were just getting started,  
Really learning how to try.  
You had taught us everything;  
Then you taught us how to die.

We were in great distress,  
Wanting your personal form to see,  
But the anxiety would stop,  
For you left your body in the GBC.

You gave us your word  
Māyā will not infiltrate  
If we continue to remain pure  
And cooperate.

Now we must continue our duty  
And go in every direction  
To bring the conditioned souls  
Under Your Divine Grace’s protection.

*Your servants at New Orleans Yātrā*

## New Talavan

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this auspicious day of your appearance we try to remember you, your pastimes, instructions, desires and our personal association we had with you. But simply remembering is not enough-we take this day to renew our commitment to serving you. We want to continue our lives as dedicated servants, not that now that you have gone from our vision, we can go back to sleep.

You opened our eyes with the torchlight of transcendental knowledge and you inspired us to surrender to the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement be Lord Caitanya, Kṛṣṇa Himself. As the years go by, we desperately and pray that we will not compromise or decrease our devotional to you. Rather, we want to become more and more advanced and enlivened and consigned to fulfilling your mission of expanding true spiritual culture in the world. And as long as we keep our faith in your books, your GBC representatives, your matured disciples an ISKCON, then this remembrance of you will be fruitful and productive in terms of our advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, your pleasure with our service and the conditioned souls' returning back to Godhead.

In New Talavan we are aspiring to realize the next phase of this movement to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. In August 1977 you were on your way to Gītā-nāgarī to show us how to develop these farm projects but Kṛṣṇa had other plans, and you never came. Now we must struggle manifest your dream of a Kṛṣṇa conscious society based on the land and the cows, and we cannot refer to a concise blueprint or specific manifesto you might have given us had you stayed with us longer. So we always recall your encouraging words to us residents of New Talavan, Spoken in Dallas, 1975:

Remain enthusiastic,  
Be confident of success,  
Follow the regulative principles,  
Be straight forward,  
Keep the association of devotees,  
Be patient,  
Do not become morose,  
And Kṛṣṇa will surely help you.

*Your servants,*
