## New Vrindaban

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Sometimes in your Vyāsa-pūjā book we reprint a homage given by you to Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati in 1936. You began as follows:

*sākṣād-haritvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*yuktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*  
*kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī caraṇāravindam*

**You went on to translate as follows: "In the revealed scriptures declared that the spiritual master should be worshiped like the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and this injunction is obeyed by pure devotee of the Lord. The spiritual master is the most confidential servant Lord. Thus, let us offer our respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of our spiritual master."**

This is how you chose to start your Vyāsa-pūjā, so we understand this type of worship leads to victory. We are trying to make that instruction our life and soul; we are trying to make you our life and soul.

We are not very intelligent or advanced, but we are working with faith in you and Kṛṣṇa. Perhaps there are some who are expert scholars of Vedic literatures, astrology and other sciences, but all we know is you and your books.

*ei kali-kāle āra nāhi kona dharma*  
*vaiṣṇava, vaiṣṇava-śāstra, ei kahe marma*  
*Madhya 9.362*

**"In this age of Kali there are no genuine religious principles. There are only Vaiṣṇava devotees and Vaiṣṇava devotional literatures; this is the sum and substance of everything."**

There is another Source of inspiration for us-i.e. your visions, dreams, and your specific instructions on how to establish the consciousness movement. Being the confidential servant of Kṛṣṇa, are *tri-kāla-jña;* you are able to see past, present and future. We thought we were joining some small club, but you initiated us into Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement and gave us glimpses into the future, describing the scope and success of the spiritual world. Even in the sixties, when ISKCON was a newborn infant, you could already see your temples. "There are only separated by time,” you seriously said, you also described your dreams about the future of New Vrindaban.

You described New Vrindaban as a jungle, but said it would become a Kṛṣṇa conscious town. Now we are on the United States map-the first Kṛṣṇa conscious city.

You instructed us to develop New Vrindaban on the level of Tirupati; now hundreds of thousands of pilgrims visit New Vrindaban each year.

In your room in Māyāpur, you said, "All over the world my disciples are creating temples to worship Kṛṣṇa, but Kīrtanānanda Swami and you all are building a palace to worship me. This is the proper understanding of our philosophy." Now we see you situated radiantly in your Western-world *Puṣpa-samādhi*, giving *darśana* to half a million souls (this year) and blessing us with your presence.

*ārādhanānāṁ sarveṣāṁ viṣṇor ārādhanaṁ param*  
*tasmāt parataraṁ devi tadīyānāṁ samarcanam*

**"Of all types of worship, worship of Lord Viṣnu is best, and better than the worship of Lord Viṣnu is the worship of His devotee, the Vaiṣṇava." (Padma Purāṇa)**

You even said that, "In our Kṛṣṇa conscious movement we inaugurated the New Vṛndāvana activities wherein devotees are always engaged in the transcendental loving service of the Lord, and this is not different from Goloka Vṛndāvana." (*Cc. Madhya* 3.35) It is only your devotion that will attract Kṛṣṇa, and if we try to serve you, then Kṛṣṇa may come to make New Vrindaban into Goloka Vrindaban.

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*ahaṁ bhakta-parādhīno*  
*hy asvatantra iva dvija*  
*sādhubhir grasta-hṛdayo*  
*bhaktair bhakta-jana-priyaḥ*  
*SB 9.4.63*

**"The Supreme Personality of Godhead said to the** ***brāhmaṇa:*** **I am completely under the control of My devotees. Indeed, I am not at all independent. Because My devotees are completely devoid of material desires, I sit only within the cores of their hearts. What to speak of My devotee, even those who are devotees of My devotees are very dear to Me."**

Because you are an intimate associate of Lord Caitanya, your instructions and visions are the blueprints we need to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness for the next ten thousand years. The *ācāryas* and devotees of this movement will refer to your plans for centuries. By worshiping you we have been blessed by Kṛṣṇa to manifest some of these plans Vrindaban. We've placed you at the top, and we'll keep worshiping there, and Kṛṣṇa will certainly turn your dreams into reality. Then when you take us all back to Godhead, we can proudly say, “See what Śrīla Prabhupāda has done-he is the greatest!"

## Norman, Uklanoma

*My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. I must begin this Vyāsa-pūjā homage by thanking you for being so kind as to give me this preaching assignment of delivering the fallen, conditioned souls here in the state of Oklahoma. It sometimes seems inconceivable how here in this abode of slow, dull-headed people who proudly call themselves "Okies" that anyone could take an interest in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Yet I am experiencing that somehow or other people are coming to our Kṛṣṇa Yoga Center, they are purchasing your books, taking *prasādam* and chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa. Surely this is the inconceivable attractiveness of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, whose weapon for subduing the demons is His beauty. I am realizing, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that the more I can become a pure devotee, the more Lord Caitanya's potency will act through me. Therefore I am begging, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you kindly empower me to become a perfect *gosvāmī,* or master of the senses, so that I may be able to serve you in the most effective way.

Even though I have been sent here alone, actually I am not all alone, for you and my Godbrothers, by the potency of ś*abda-brahma,* are here with me. Otherwise, how is it possible that I could maintain my Kṛṣṇa consciousness? By preaching work I am gradually creating through your mercy a circle of associates who are nicely assisting me in my *saṅkīrtana* activities. Many devotees feel a need to get married so that they can have a family, but I am experiencing that by marrying myself to preaching I am able to create a bigger family in a much quicker and easier way.

As I hear the preaching reports coming in from around the world I become very excited about how this entire planet is actually becoming transformed into a Kṛṣṇa conscious world right before our very eyes. It is not inconceivable that with our present enthusiastic preaching mood the whole world could become surrendered to ISKCON within thirty years. So please empower us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to strictly follow your guidelines, and mercifully bestow Kṛṣṇa consciousness on everyone that we meet. In such a mood we shall always be riveted to your lotus feet, with no chance of ever falling down.

We beg you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to please send your special blessings to us, your growing spiritual family of humble servants here in the land of the Okies.

*Your servants,*  
Saṅkarṣaṇa dāsa / Bhakta John / Bhaktin Nancy

## Ottawa

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. For those of us here who are your initiated disciples, many years have passed since you handed us our sanctified *japa-mālā* beads, gave us our new spiritual names, whispered in our ears the sacred Gāyatri *mantra.* We read in your books that to practice *bhakti-yoga* one must (1) accept a bona fide spiritual master, (2) accept initiation from him, (3) serve him, and (4) receive instruction from the spiritual master. So obediently we executed these first items, thinking now that we have completed these preliminary steps we can continue on to higher matters. Gradually it is dawning on us: the gravity of accepting *dīkṣā* from a bona fide spiritual master. A total commitment of body, mind and words for the satisfaction of Śrī *Guru*deva is the most essential ingredient for achieving the supreme goal*-Kṛṣṇa-prema.* We now understand the absolute necessity of regular and submissive hearing of the message of Śrīla Vyāsadeva from the lips of the spiritual master and how this brings about a mature seriousness to our lives. Hearing the commentaries on the scriptures by the spiritual master unlocks the soul's bondage to ignorance by dispelling all his doubts and attachments.

These first items in the series of devotional practices have been listed first by Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī because they form the foundation of a devotee's progress back to Godhead. A deep realization of this truth will assure the disciple of success and satisfaction in his spiritual pursuits. A lack of understanding will frustrate the aspirant devotee, because this is the root of the offensive attitude which tramples the creeper of devotion.

We pray that on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā you will bless us with a greater appreciation for the fortunate position that we now find ourselves in-disciples and grand disciples of A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda. Our only desire is that we never stop appreciating the mercy that has been bestowed upon us by Your Divine Grace.

*Your servants of ISKCON Ottawa,*

## Panama

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

I have been blessed with the opportunity to worship you on this most holy day of your appearance, Yyāsa pūjā by writing this offering in glorification of you. Please help me to find the adequate words to worship you. I cannot find those words, but I have great faith that this process that you so kindly have given to suffering mankind, Kṛṣṇa consciousness, will help us develop the qualities of a real devotee. One of the qualities of a devotee is that he is poetic.

We can remember other great devotees like Prahlāda Mahārāja, who after being touched by Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva spoke such beautiful words in glorification of the Lord, and Dhruva Mahārāja, who also spoke such beautiful words in glorification of the Lord after being touched by the Lord. Although the Lord gave them the intelligence by which they could glorify the Lord, still they used that intelligence only to glorify the Lord, and not for their own benefit. Therefore I know that all I am made of is your causeless mercy. The only way that we can glorify you is if you give us the intelligence to do so. Therefore we pray to you sincerely to give us the intelligence by which we can properly glorify you. If not with words, at least we can glorify you by the distribution of your books.

*Your servants from Panama Mandira*

## Perth

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

Whenever there is a decline in religious practice and a rise of irreligion, Kṛṣṇa descends to this material world. He also sends HIS empowered representative. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are such a representative, and through your instructions we can approach the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Because of the sinful, independent mentality of the living entities in this material world, they have turned their backs on their original father , Kṛṣṇa, and so Kṛṣṇa has very kindly sent you to remind them that they are all *nitya-Kṛṣṇa-dāsa,* eternal servants of Kṛṣṇa. You have taught us how we can again revive our original dormant God consciousness- simply chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, take *prasādam,* dance and be happy. What could be simpler? Also, you have given us beautiful spiritual music and a vast treasure house of Vedic wisdom, with which we can come to an understanding of ourselves and the world around us.

For these wonderful benedictions, how can we, who are sons and daughters of *mlecchas,* ever begin to repay you? We remain eternally indebted to you because you have saved us all from material life.

Now we see how this transcendental ISKCON movement is spreading, even to the remotest corners of the globe. We pray that just as you have transformed the lives of so many people by your purity and devotion to Kṛṣṇa, we will somehow or other be able to firmly establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness here in Perth, which is the epitome of a typical sinful Kali-yuga city.

*Your eternal servants in Perth,*

## Philadelphia

*Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

In this auspicious occasion of your appearance day, we, your disciples and granddisciples serving in Philadelphia, wish to convey to you a simple message of gratitude, because you have saved us from the miserable material existence and brought us to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We were lost in nihilistic confusion, and you gave meaning and direction to our lives. We were in a deathlike trance of material darkness, and you awoke us to the light of transcendental reality. We were full of anxieties due to forgetfulness of our eternal nature and of Kṛṣṇa, and you gave us peace. We were in ignorance, and you enlightened us. We were in a hopeless condition, and you gave us hope. We were so unhappy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and you made us happy and satisfied. All that we have become, we owe to you.

Reading back over these words, we feel foolish and embarrassed, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because they present only a pale image of what we want to say. When we ponder the magnitude of your gift to us, we can only feel frustration trying to express our gratitude through the medium of the English language. It is a mundane language that cannot comprehend the depth and subtlety of spiritual existence. As the *gopīs* condemned Lord Brahmā as inept for creating eyelids that blink (momentarily obscuring the ecstatic vision of the Lord), we curse this English language as a pitifully crippled vehicle for articulating our humble and heartfelt appreciation for your great mercy upon us. Mundane language makes a sad mockery of spiritual sentiments. Language seems to turn our most cherished feelings into tired, pale babblings. Please look beyond this dark curtain of words and examine our inner thoughts. Like the all-pervading Supersoul, gaze deeply into our hearts. There you will find a celestial symphony of *prabhupāda-bhakti-*ecstatic devotion for you. There you will hear the sweetest poetry-words fashioned from *cintāmaṇi-*describing and exulting in your transcendental glories. There you will behold your own holy image reflected and enshrined in the sanctum sanctorum of our hearts.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we all love you dearly and beg from you the boon that we may remain in your holy association, under your magnanimous protection, in your sacred service, eternally. We think that there is no higher perfection than this. We cry out from the depths of our being for this wonderful benediction.

*Your servants at New Nīlācala,*

## Portugal

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Once a famous man in the material world stated in praise of some courageous soldiers who had given their lives in the service of their country, "Never in history I have so few done so much for so many." However, your appearance in this material world has opened our eyes. To save or protect the body only, forgetting the soul, is ignorance. Like one retrieving the cloak of a drowning man, leaving him to flounder in the sea, members of our modern society blinded by Kali have forgotten the soul and absorbed themselves fully in the exploitation of their temporary material bodies. Their various attempts to save, protect, beautify or enjoy this body win for themselves lavish praise or titles of honor in the fields of science, medicine, art or militaristic endeavor.

But by Kṛṣṇa's grace we had become a little suspicious of the so-called value they placed upon this body and material life, and your divine appearance in this world and specifically in our lives confirmed our doubts in matter and those dedicated to its exclusive adulation. You once said, "I can make anyone completely happy; all that is needed is that he give up his misery ." In your wonderful way you gave us wonderful Kṛṣṇa, and in exchange for our renunciation of misery you gave us immortality.

In the Kṛṣṇa book, in the story of Kāliya the serpent, there is one beautiful example of Kṛṣṇa's mystic potency. "Because the water was so poisonous, the boys and cows became visibly affected immediately after drinking. They suddenly fell down on the ground, apparently dead. Then Kṛṣṇa, who is the life of all lives, simply cast His merciful glance over them, and all the boys and cows regained consciousness and began to look at one another with great astonishment. They could understand that by drinking the water of the Yamuna they had died and that the merciful glance of Kṛṣṇa restored their life. Thus they appreciated the mystic power of Kṛṣṇa, who is known as Yogeśvara, the master of all mystic *yogīs."*

This same life-giving glance, so astonishing to the cowherd boys, is now available to the living entities in general through the presentation of your books and the loud chanting of Kṛṣṇa's holy names. The dissemination of *Kṛṣṇa-kīrtana.* both written and sung, is gradually reviving the lives of all those who have drunk poison willfully in this miserable material world. Witnessing this miracle has become a daily affair for your disciples and granddisciples, and like the cowherd boys with Kṛṣṇa we become increasingly amazed by the power of your faultless glorification of the Supreme Personality of Godhead and His devotional service. This is your mystic potency, and it has changed our lives and the lives of countless generations to come. Therefore without hesitation we can glorify you unlimitedly, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because in comparison to you in the history of mankind, "Never has one man done so much for so many." All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants,*

Praghoṣa dāsa / Anantadeva dāsa / Locana dāsa / Jṇānāgni dāsa / Bhakta Paulo / Bhakta Quim / Rādhāṅga-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Sonia / Bhaktin Paula / Bhaktin Isabel

## Puerto Rico

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We offer our respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having surrendered at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.

On this day celebrating your merciful appearance in this dark world, we have the opportunity to offer you some words of glorification. This is not such a substantial presentation, since Kṛṣṇa Himself has already made you famous all over the three worlds, where countless thousands of men are already chanting your glories. However, it serves as an important opportunity to purify ourselves by meditating deeply on our relationship with you and our responsibilities to you. In the beginning years of ISKCON, so many men said they wanted this relationship and promised to fulfill the responsibilities of that relationship forever. In those most relishable years, you taught us everything that we needed. You taught us how to live together peacefully in Kṛṣṇa's temples, how to maintain the Deity worship nicely, how to prepare, honor and distribute wonderful *Kṛṣṇa-prasādam,* how to profusely distribute your books all over the world, and how to chant the holy names of God in the streets every day. This was the foundation that you built your ISKCON movement on. So many other activities could and would be done in devotional service, many of them being necessary to facilitate the basic foundation, and many of them nicely augmenting the basic foundation. However, you did show very clearly how to push on Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement by your own personal example. Having some steady service in that vast network of transcendental activity was the basis of our relationship with you. And you gave us the eternal responsibility of maintaining these basic programs and never letting them decrease.

But where did so many of these men go? Where are those shaven-headed devotees who made up your *saṅkīrtana* army? You said that in the war between the devotees and *māyā,* we would suffer many casualties. But at the same time, you taught us that the simple process of chanting, dancing and *eating prasādam* in the association of pure devotees would certainly bring love for Kṛṣṇa. So many men became casualties in this war because their consciousness drifted away from your basic program into so many other things like family life, business, music, different sociopolitical considerations, etc. But how can there be any taste in chewing that old rubbish which we chewed for so many lifetimes? What can be more satisfying to the tongue than first-class *prasādam* offered to the Deity in the temple? Can sex life ever compare to the exhilarating bodily ecstasy of dancing in a raging *kīrtana?* Doesn't Kṛṣṇa give peace of mind and good intelligence to a man who dedicates his life to pushing on this *saṅkīrtana* movement? Who can lack any of the *real* necessities of life if he is determined to always live in the temples you built for us? The basic program that you gave us *is* the higher taste in Kṛṣṇa consciousness; but out of ignorance, we give up that sweet and simple relationship with you. In a frustrated attempt to taste something sweet, a jaundiced person will stop eating sugar before he is cured of his disease and go back to eating things that will make his condition worse again.

So on this auspicious day, let us offer you the most valuable words that we can. Let those words take the form of that same vow that we made so many years ago: to keep ourselves spiritually pure by carefully following the orders you gave at the time of initiation; to always remain absorbed in the ecstatic devotional activities that you gave us as the basis of our relationship with you; to carefully maintain this ISKCON movement as you created it and left it in our charge. If we can fulfill the words of this promise, we know that you will obtain the highest pleasure from this Vyāsa-pūjā offering. In this way, we can hope to remain your eternally faithful disciples and granddisciples.

*Your servants,*

## Rio de Janeiro

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**"Even half a moment's association with pure devotees within this world of birth and death is a priceless treasure for any man."** ***(Śr*****ī*****mad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.2.30)**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

This place is full of illusion. People here are always trying to enjoy their senses to the fullest extent, and in this city the "heavenly" atmosphere created by very powerful demons is there only to try to trap us into forgetting our real self-interest-eternal service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Lord of the heart. But your mercy is so great that you have agreed to always be present in the form of your instructions and transcendental books, only to give us always the hint that we are not our bodies, we are not God, and this material world is a temporary place full of miseries, thus destroying our blunt ignorance, then planting in our hearts the seed of devotion, of love of God.

We cannot estimate the volume of the gift of your association. We can only be satisfied to accept the result of your so-short stay in this *māyā-dhāma:* thousands and thousands of pure devotees, living only to bring you near to everyone with your presence, your desire.

Please Śrīla Prabhupāda, Patita-pāvana and Jagad-*guru*, give your causeless mercy, blessing us to become like those pure devotees and to live only for spreading this movement of Lord Caitanya.

*Your lowly servants of Rio de Janeiro Yātrā,*

## St. Louis

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet, which have already laid the foundation and framework for a spiritual home that could happily house the entire planet. All glories to you on this most transcendental day for all persons, for the next ten thousand years.

It is stated in the ś*āstra,*

*ārādhanānāṁ sarveṣāṁ viṣṇor ārādhanaṁ param*  
*tasmāt parataraṁ devi tadīyānāṁ samarcanam*

Of all the different tyes of worship Lord Śiva states, worship of Viṣṇu is best. But even better than worshiping Viṣṇu is the worship of the pure devotee or things in relation to Viṣṇu and His pure devotee.

I remember that during the festival in Vṛndāvana in 1976, in the mornings you would take your morning walk in Ramaṇa-retī with the *sannyāsīs* and GBC men. One *brahmacārī,* Praghoṣa, was sneaking on those walks, and I was very envious. The site of the walk was in the distance, so only those who knew the place could go on the walk. This *brahmacāri* got ready to go one morning, and I criticized, "You always hog all the nectar, Praghoṣa. Tell me where you're going!" Due to my offensively demanding nature, he said, " All right, come on, but don't tell anybody." We took a ricksha to the forest site, and the walk had already begun. You were talking of how the *karmīs* are so much like animals that they even name their paraphernalia after animals. One devotee said "Jaguar." Another said "Pinto." Then you said "Greyhound," and Praghoṣa said, "But we use the Greyhound to preach, Śrīla Prabhu- pāda." "That is the perfection," you replied! Then later you asked one Indian *sannyāsī* what were the berries on a certain tree. He replied that they were edible and sages used to live in the forest and simply exist on these berries. You said that our men should be able to do like this-chant all day, eat some berries, and once a week go to the village and beg a few *capātīs.* Immediately some devotees responded, "But Prabhupāda, we have to preach, and. …" "That's all right," you said. "But we should be able to chant all day Hare Kṛṣṇa, eat a few berries, and once a week go to the village and beg a few *capātīs.* " The whole walk you had been holding one small bouquet of flowers that was given, and occasionally you held them to your nose to smell them.

When the walk ended, everyone headed back to the mandir for *Bhāgavatam* class. Because Praghoṣa and I took a ricksha back, we were late, and the Jaya Rādhā-Mādhava prayers had already begun, and apparently you had given someone in the front that bouquet. Praghoṣa and I sat in the back, and after a few minutes someone handed me this beautiful small bouquet. I recognized it and immediately gave it to Praghoṣa. He, upon understanding that this bouquet was the one from the morning walk, to my surprise and amazement ripped it into two pieces, handing half back to me. Then with the fervor of finding a sweet ball from the Lord's plate, he ate his half of the bouquet, smiling all the while. Although I materially calculated how it might be rough on my digestive tract, I followed suit and ate the bunch of flowers in about three blissful gulps. We knew that we had been blessed by some wonderful *tadīya* of Kṛṣṇa's greatest *tadīya.*

It is said that every seven years the old cells in the body die and brand new cells are formed, and this *tadīya* was given in Vṛndāvana in 1976. Therefore we are lamenting that the cells containing this *tadīya* (externally speaking) are probably gone this year, 1983. Of course, spiritually speaking, the benefit is eternal, and spirit never fades. But still, we are begging for some special mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because we are the most fallen and are completely dependent ultimately on you. Please send that mercy, and allow us to assist you, birth after birth, in your spreading of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*Your eternal servants.*

## Salvador, Brazil

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

The world was full of atheists, agnostics and all sorts of foolish people, all against the principles enunciated by the *ācāryas.* Thus the world was overcome by the opposition, and the result was that we were suffering the calamities imposed by the illusory energy, *māyā.*

Seeing this terrible situation, Your Divine Grace was afflicted, and despite any inconvenience, you came to bring the medicine for this diseased society-the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare, Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu's mission.

Your ocean of mercy was overflowing the whole world. You preached the holy name in all directions and woke up everyone sleeping.

Today , this great and sacred responsibility to turn the fortune and fate of the distressed has been given to your followers. You want devotional service to God to go on being distributed in this way; but the poor materialists still suffer.

O most merciful! Please give us a chance so that, somehow or other, we can assist you in this big mission. Although unqualified, just by meditating on your mercy, which is scientific and authorized, our hearts become filled with love and we become brave enough to fight in this battle. Please grant us a place under the shade of your lotus feet.

*From your most fallen grandchildren,*

Advaita dāsa / Hari-cakra dāsa / Rāma-preṣtha dāsa / Nitya-sukhī dāsa / Śrīdāmi dāsa / Gaṅgi-śuddha dāsa / Bhakta Howwer / Bhakta Antonio / Bhakta Arnaldo / Bhakta George / Arundhatī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Lea / Bhaktin Marcia

## San Diego

*śrī-guru-caraṇe rati, ei se uttama gati,*  
*je prasāde pure sarva āśā*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our prostrate obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

For how many millions upon millions of lifetimes have we been rotting in this material world, struggling under the massive weight of our sins? We had no hope of deliverance or even the slightest idea of what our actual position was.

Then you appeared, the self-effulgent pure devotee of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Your appearance in this world was unlike ours. You had no desire to meddle in the mundane affairs of this dark and frightful place. Your only desire was to deliver the conditioned souls. Your coming here was your causeless mercy upon us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most wonderful mystic! By your all-attractive qualities you've captured our hearts. Just as Śrī Caitanya Māhāprabhu exhibited His divine effulgence to attract the attention of the impersonalists, so you also manifested innumerable transcendental qualities and pastimes to attract us. Your qualities are all sublime and pure.

We remember how you attracted us-

- by your pure love for Śrī-Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa,
- by your full surrender to and implicit faith in your *Guru* Māhāraja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī,
- by your compassion for all living entities,
- by your bold and dynamic preaching,
- by the delicious *prasādam* you taught us to prepare,
- by your ability to do the impossible,
- by your glance laden with benediction for your disciples,
- by your supremely blissful smile,
- by your pure chanting of the holy names,
- by the charming and graceful movements of your body,
- by your books, which are like the sun, illuminating our hearts with Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and
- by your ISKCON society of faithful devotees, who've accepted you as their life and soul.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, there was no question of us ever taking up the principles of spiritual life and rejecting *māyā's* invitation to enjoy if we didn't have your transcendental association. And we must always remember that if we give up your association (i.e. your *vāṇī)* then we will once again have no potency to resist *māyā's* pulling.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the benediction that the vision of your transcendental form, pastimes and instructions will forever be resplendent in our hearts. Let our attraction for you increase more and more, until it turns into pure *love-Prabhupāda-prema.* That will be the perfection that fulfills all of our desires. Jaya Prabhupāda! Jaya *Guru*deva!

*Your servants.*

## San Francisco

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All Glories to Śrī *Guru* and Gaurāṅga!!!

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your holy lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the empowered incarnation for spreading Lord Caitanya's movement all over the world. By your mercy even the lowest of mankind like us have an opportunity to develop love of Kṛṣṇa, the highest perfection attainable. By actually satisfying you, no obstacle can dare to hinder us on our progressive march back home, back to Godhead.

But are you pleased with us? This is the question. Either individually or collectively, the whole essence of our success or failure in spiritual life rests on this point. Nor is there any way to circumvent this test. Not by dint of austerity, construction of huge temples or accumulation of knowledge. No! Not even by chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa or distributing your books are we automatically assured of that goal. Yes, even these devotional activities, in order to fully please you, must be performed in a humble service mood, with full awareness of, and gratitude for, your direct mercy-the mercy of your kindly engaging us in this way.

Otherwise there is every chance of stumbling into the pitfall of pride, which deludes us into thinking that we are due the credit of these accomplishments. Help us to avoid this arrogance of carrying an inflated view of ourselves, which hampers our cooperative mood and prevents us from presenting a united front in our struggle against *māyā.* Let us not disregard your final instruction: "You'll show your love for me by how you cooperate together."

*Your servants in San Francisco*

## Sāo Luīz, Brazil

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupada,*

On this most blessed day of your appearance, we would like to offer you our most sincere homages.

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I was born in the darkness of ignorance, which is this material body, but my spiritual master opened my darkened eyes with the torch of knowledge. Therefore, I offer him my most humble obeisances.**

We, your followers from this most remote end of the world, in Sāo Luīz, Brazil, would like to render our respectful homages on this most auspicious day, with our humble and sincere attempt, though still imperfect, to be able to distribute Kṛṣṇa consciousness unlimitedly in this farmost state in Brazil. We pray that we can please you in this way, and then we will be able to chant the holy name purely.

By remembering you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, one immediately remembers Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. That is because you take us to the ocean of ecstatic love of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. You show us the service to Them.

On this event of your appearance day, we feel separation from you within our hearts. We cannot bear it. Tears flow from our eyes like torrents of rain, and in your absence, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we feel completely alone in the world.

I just cried when you got aboard the *Jaladuta,* as described in the *Līlāmṛta* Volume I. There was no one to bid you farewell. There was not a huge *kīrtana,* nor flowers, nor service unto your lotus feet. I just became sad.

Precious are the moments we are Kṛṣṇa conscious. Remembering you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is reviving our poor, sleeping consciousness.

We are so fallen, enchanted by *māyā.* To live in this illusion causes pain in the heart. Please, please, sleeping consciousness, please wake up!

We must remember Śrīla Prabhupāda and directly follow him. Love for him goes directly to Kṛṣṇa.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, read this offering from your fallen servants,

Puruṣ-purāṇa dāsa / Kṛṣṇa-Murāri dāsa / Bhakta Marcos / Bhakta Geraldo / Tattvavit-devī dāsī

## Sāo Paulo, Brazil

And then he came, sweet as a rose, hard as a thunderbolt.  
Sweet for those surrendered. Sweet for everyone.  
His sweetness was as big as his mercy to enlighten all conditioned souls.  
Hard he was for those unsurrendered, or for anyone of us who would  
dare to challenge Kṛṣṇa's supremacy.  
Hard he was to defeat them all.  
And then he came, as Kṛṣṇa’s causeless mercy,  
he appeared among us,  
enacted his līlā as a transcendental avatāra and  
told everyone he met, " My duty is to preach about surrender to Kṛṣṇa. Whether you take it  
or not is up to you. I am just preaching about Kṛṣṇa’s glories. "  
And because of his greatness, thousands now are following him;  
thousands now are partaking of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, going back to Godhead.  
And then he came, swiftly as a needle  
to penetrate anyone’s heart.  
Such a great soul is very rare.  
Still not everyone knows about his exalted position.  
Some people respect him, but that is not enough.  
All of them-they must know who he is, they must surrender unto him.  
That is the real universal brotherhood, that everyone should  
accept Kṛṣṇa consciousness by following Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
And then he came, with such an important message to whoever would be there to listen.  
But just speaking for those who came, it would be only "small time. "  
That is why he was so bold, to force people to be attracted and come.  
He endeavored so much to distribute millions of books, construct gor-  
geous temples, open so many farm communities, start so many projects.  
All under Kṛṣṇa’s victory flag.  
And he went like a bold soldier in a big battle to fight against the enemy māyā.  
And because of his boldness the world is being saved.  
And then he came, a swanlike devotee, giving the mercy of the bhaktivedāntas.  
But as he said, he would never leave.  
He would live forever by his divine and eternal instructions.  
And the followers live With him, safely inside the  
transcendental boat that takes us back to Kṛṣṇa.  
He will be the captain of this boat forever,  
otherwise it would be only chaos.  
And then he came, as Lord Caitanya had predicted it.  
He gave us the example of a perfect leader,  
because he himself was a perfect follower of the paramparā.  
Now it is up to us  
to preach his message and let him be known to all.  
I, for myself; am a cripple-minded person.  
I just beg to have the mercy of Prabhupāda's servants,  
to follow them and help them serve Śrīla Prabhupāda.  
Because, as Prabhupāda explained, we have to be  
the servant of the servant.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
on this most auspicious event,  
we just beg Kṛṣṇa so that He can enlighten and protect us  
to be always able to follow you.  
Māyā is so big, there is always the danger of being led astray.  
But for those who have surrendered unto you, they can conquer māyā.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
we beg for your mercy.  
But by this same mercy of yours, we know that your mercy  
does not come when we just sit around, waiting for that.  
You have shown us that the mercy of the guru only comes  
when we are surrendered to follow you.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
we fall at your lotus feet  
in whatever little attempt we may make in broadcasting your glories.

*All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!*

*Your servants from Sāo Paulo, Brazil,*

## Scandinavia

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

In time the world will appreciate the greatness of your achievement in singlehandedly spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness worldwide in a few short years. It was not merely a superhuman task, but an impossible one. The proof of its impossibility is that you alone could plan and execute the task perfectly; to others it was simply inconceivable. In this way you are reflecting the opulence of your dearest Lord, master and friend, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who is *asamordhva-*no one is greater than Him, and no one is equal to Him.

But your success in spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement is the external sign of your greatness. Your real greatness is that the Supreme Absolute Truth, Personality of Godhead, works through you unlimitedly.

The task that you achieved was to spread the very highest perfection of love of Godhead throughout the whole world in Kali-yuga. Only once in every day of Lord Brahmā is that possible, and then only by the grace of Lord Caitanya. Caitanya Mahāprabhu is more merciful than Lord Kṛṣṇa, because Lord Kṛṣṇa insists on full surrender, whereas Lord Caitanya is so ecstatically absorbed in *Kṛṣṇa-prema* that He distributes love of Godhead indiscriminately. Lord Nityānanda, the original spiritual master, is more merciful than Lord Caitanya, for only Nityānanda Prabhu could deliver Jagāi and Mādhāi.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are even greater than Lord Nityānanda, by His desire. Śrī Kṛṣṇa Himself has spoken to the whole world through your purports and glorified you above all others: "Similarly, if one is true to Gaura-Nitāi's service in the disciplic succession, he can even excel Nityānanda Prabhu's service. This is the process of disciplic succession. Nityānanda Prabhu delivered Jagāi and Mādhāi, but a servant of Nityānanda Prabhu, by His grace, can deliver many thousands of Jagāis and Mādhāis. This is the special benediction of the disciplic succession."

Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu made the subcontinent of India Kṛṣṇa conscious, but He only left eight written verses. His intimate servants, the six Gosvāmīs, composed voluminous literatures, but they remained mostly in Vṛndāvana. By Mahāprabhu's unfathomable will, the *saṅkīrtana* movement that He founded almost dāsappeared; less than five hundred years after His appearance, its only real manifestation within the material world was your unquenchable faith in the instructions of your spiritual master.

Only you could tour the world, untouched by its unparalleled atheistic materialism, and at the same time compose a library of transcendental literature, deliver thousands from extreme degradation and lay the foundations of a spiritual empire which is destined to endure for thousands of years. Who else could do this?

*Vede gāya jāhāra carito:* the Vedic scriptures sing the glories of the *ācārya,* but factually we cannot find anyone of your stature in the Vedic literatures. The Vedic literatures only hint at the glories of Caitanya Mahāprabhu, but leave unsung the glories of His confidential servant, who by the Lord's will accomplished what He Himself did not do. The mind is stunned when it tries to contemplate this wonder. Surely even Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself is astounded.

What is your secret? We may read it in *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta.* After Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmi amazed Lord Caitanya by composing a verse that perfectly expressed the Lord's most intimate feelings of separation from Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Caitanya Mahāprabhu showed the verse to Svarūpa Dāmodara Gosvāmi, asking him how Rūpa Gosvāmi could have understood His mind. In your purport you comment that you received a similar blessing from Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī when you composed a verse that perfectly expressed his mission. "How could we have understood the intentions of Śrīla Prabhupāda?" you ask rhetorically.

Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmi explains your position in the next two verses: "Śrīla Svarūpa Dāmodara Gosvāmi replied to Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu: 'If Rupa Gosvāmi can understand Your mind and intentions, he must have Your Lordship's special benediction.' The Lord said: I was so pleased with Rupa Gosvāmi that I embraced him and bestowed upon him all necessary potencies for preaching the *bhakti* cult.' "

This, then, is the essence of your greatness, Śrīla Prabhupāda. It is not just that you achieved something that was impossible for anyone else, but rather that, out of all the unlimitedly exalted and intimate devotees in the Supreme Lord's unlimited transcendental entourage, you were the devotee that He singled out to execute His most confidential mission in the material world. You are the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead.

Therefore we have absolute confidence that in bowing down at your lotus feet and taking your instructions to our hearts as our life and soul, we have come to the topmost position in the totality of the material and spiritual manifestations. There is simply nothing higher or greater than this: *tasmāt parataraṁ devī tadīyānāṁ samarcanam.* Greater than worship of the Supreme Lord is worship of those who are *tadīyānām,* dear to Him. Of all those who are dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, the preacher is *priya-kṛttama.* the most dear, and of all preachers, you are the most confidential. Therefore worshiping you by hearing and executing your instructions is the absolute perfection of existence.

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ*  
*paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande*  
*paramānanda-mādhavam*  
*Madhya 17.80*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we, your disciples and granddisciples of the Scandinavian Yātrā, are blind because we cannot see you and your ISKCON in pure transcendence. We are dumb because we cannot glorify you or chant the holy name offenselessly or preach with complete purity. We are lame because we cannot execute your mission. Therefore our only recourse is to cling to your lotus feet with whatever humility and sincerity we can muster, by your limitless grace, and pray for Your Grace to fulfill your desires for us. You have said that the father likes to be defeated by the child, and that the grandfather is even more merciful than the father . We are confident that if we can only please you by our sincere desire to please you, you will act on the Lord's behalf and impart *bhakti-śakti* to those who want only this and nothing more.

After all, you want to see Vaiṣṇava culture triumph over the misguided civilization that the demons have set up, and you have left it up to us to continue with your mission, so we can only beg you to give us the necessary potency. We have no other means of sustenance.

Today, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is the anniversary of your blessed appearance. We can simply offer our pitifuny inadequate appreciation of your glories, as viewed from the depths of our wormburrow, and pray that we, may sometime become your confidential assistants, in however humble a capacity.

If there is one favor that we may ask you today, it is this: ISKCON is your body, and as such, it should work together as a potent unit. You have told us that you will know we love you when you see us cooperating together. This is most difficult because you bestowed your mercy on the worst products of the age of quarrel and hypocrisy. We must confess that we have not done our duty in this regard, and our efforts to please you have turned to ashes. Please purify our hearts of the material desires that make us prey on our Godbrothers instead of glorifying and serving them. Give us the ability to see the root of dissension and pluck it out forever, so that your ISKCON may become truly glorious. We should preach only to glorify the Supreme Lord and you, His confidential servant.

In conclusion, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can simply fall at your lotus feet, overwhelmed by our inability to glorify and serve you as you deserve. *Lokera jīvana:* you are life itself, because only you are giving us Kṛṣṇa, the supreme life. Life comes from life. We beg you to keep showering your mercy on us because, undeserving though we are, we are your children. You are our real father, our only guide and shelter. You are everything to us, and we have nothing else at all. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your servants,*

## Seattle

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**"By the mercy of the spiritual master one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement. ……”**

It is only by your causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we have been able to take to the path of devotional service.

No one invited you, but in the true spirit of the topmost Vaiṣṇava devotee, you came to the Western world to wage war against the forces of *māyā.*

You have picked us up from the darkest corner of material existence and shown us how to attain *prema-bhakti.*

You have planted the seed of love of God within our hearts and shown us how to care for our devotional creeper. Like a father guiding his young son, you carefully guided the growth of ISKCON.

On this auspicious day of your appearance, we beg that you will always keep us engaged in your service, that you will guide us in our efforts to preach and glorify the name of Rādhā-Nīlamādhava all over the world. If we are able to please you by our small endeavor, then we will consider our life's goal a success.

*Your servants in Seattle (New Kurukṣetra Dhāma),*

## South Africa

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our worthless obeisances at your lotus feet. It is our great pleasure each year to write a Vyāsa-pūjā offering, glorlfymg your divine self and your unlimited qualities and accomplishments. One might think that each year it would become easier to express our appreciation of you, but it seems that, as Kṛṣṇa gives us more and more realization of your incomparable attributes and forces us to admit more of our own weaknesses, we become increasingly awestruck and speechless as we meditate on you.

On the other hand, that stunned feeling comes from our not knowing where to begin enumerating your glories. To us, the most astounding thing is that you came to the West all alone, against all odds, and began this movement which has saved us from the merciless grip of *māyā.* This act distinguishes you completely from your contemporaries, who were overwhelmed by just the thought of such an endeavor. Some of these less advanced souls may try to belittle your achievement by saying that they could have done it themselves or whatever, but the point is that you did it, whereas they could not even begin talking about it.

You have charted the course for our progress back to Godhead, and now all we have to do is follow the route you have chalked out. It is actually that simple. And in the meantime Kṛṣṇa places us in so many situations which give us full opportunity to realize concretely the validity of your instructions.

You have given us the secret of success in Kṛṣṇa consciousness- *vyavasāyātmikā buddhir ekeha kuru-nandana* ...: just accept the instructions of the bona fide *ācārya* as one's life and soul. Don't deviate from them one inch. This is the secret to your unprecedented success in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and will be the secret to our success if we adhere to it cent percent.

At the same time, Kṛṣṇa has let us see so vividly the terrible consequences of deviating from your path. Even a disciple in whom you entrusted some of the greatest responsibility has become shipwrecked and drowned in the perilous ocean of ideas which differ from your own.

Here in South Africa we have been battling against the waves made by the previously mentioned soul's splashing into the ocean of *māyā.* but now, as we pull our heads out of that diasaster (with the aid of your dedicated disciple Śrīla Bhagavān Goswami), we have become more determined to adhere to your life-giving instructions and help you spread the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness far and wide.

Please give us the strength to do this properly. Don't let us be dragged away by the concoctions of our whimsical minds. Allow us to engage fully in your devotional service till the end of this life, and then take us to where you are performing your pastimes.

*Your servants in South Africa,*

## Spain

Dearest Prabhupāda, you have built a house  
Where all can live  
Without anxiety if one agrees  
To serve fully, to only give.

You have taught the world the process  
Of developing love for God,  
Who is the most beautiful person,  
Kṛṣṇa we must applaud!

You have revealed to us the essential thing  
All hanker for within-  
The real pleasure of the soul,  
Pure service, beneath layers of sin,.

All those who met you saw a person  
They could accept as true  
Because in you they saw the power  
Of love for God in plain view "

You touched the soul of everyone  
Who dared to ask a question,  
Because you never stopped giving Kṛṣṇa,  
Hearing and chanting without cessation.

The rascals are all demanding;  
They can't believe unless they see.  
But your mercy is so strong  
Even the blind could see for free.

And free you were beyond all limitation  
Of body and of mind;  
Through heat and cold, disease or old age,  
You were equal, one in kind.

How many babies are found to be staring  
In trance at arcā-mūrti?  
To be organizing festivals at five years,  
Blessing all with Kṛṣṇa mercy?

How many can claim to be free from sin  
Throughout an entire life?  
No one else could come West, remaining pure,  
Without succumbing to strife.

The truth is, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were sent  
Directly by God  
To deliver His sublime message intact  
Without sparing the rod.

You taught us how to worship guru  
With full unflinching faith,  
As the single formula for success,  
The source of potency, spiritual strength.

The secret to be independent  
Is not simply to protest,  
But to become fully dependent  
On one who has the power to test.

The standards you set, so strict, so high,  
Cannot be imitated.  
Please bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda; empower us  
To follow unrestricted.

Always miles ahead, we could not grasp  
The significance of what you said and did.  
Now, years later, we're only beginning to see  
The results of seeds you hid.

Through your Bhaktivedanta purports  
You have flooded the world with Kṛṣṇa-prema.  
Soon the whole world will dance and chant  
And feast with you-relieved from its dilemma.

The promise of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi  
Carried carefully in your heart-  
Who can imagine the pleasure  
You have given the Lord from the start!

You are so dear to Kṛṣṇa,  
All your desires must come true.  
We therefore know, eternal father,  
Who’s doing-its only you!

Now we only want to please you  
By maintaining what you have given.  
And if you bless us, to increase,  
Completely fulfilling your mission.

Then with the goal in sight  
We want to join you in our next life,  
To continue to taste the topmost satisfaction  
Of associating with you, our source of life.

*Your eternal disciples and granddisciples of the Spanish Yātrā*

## State College

After the cold, dark, almost interminable winter, when the sun reappears in spring, lighting up the sky and bringing warmth and new life, it is a time of great happiness.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have brought this spring to us in the winter of our inconsolable discontent. Your mercy has magically induced transcendental flowers to bloom in this frozen Siberian wasteland of the material world.

This appearance of spring appears to be especially fortuitous, for the winter of Kali-yuga is meant to last 427 ,000 long years more; but, factually, it is the *saṅkīrtana* spring, a bright efflorescence of causeless mercy radiating from the advent of the golden sun of Lord Gaurāṅga. This quickening sunlight, your pure heart, Śrīla Prabhupāda, has reflected into Kali-yuga's darkest caverns (and taverns) wherein we pale, miserable cave-dwellers and underground men huddled, trying futilely to remain warm by the stolen heat from others' bodies and by imagination.

Having felt the warmth of the *mahā-mantra,* having learned from you *prabhāsmi śaśi-sūryayoḥ,* that the warmth of that sun, so welcome on our chill, pale faces, the bright *illumination, jñānam,* by which things are rightly understood, and the beauty and delicate fragrance of the spring flowers-that all of these are but tiny manifestations of the splendor of Lord Kṛṣṇa, the eternal cause of all causes, we can now manifest our discontent with the cloud of *māyā* in our own hearts which blocks that life-giving warmth. Especially in the winter, if a cloud covers the sun, the cold is grievously felt. But you have so kindly, so meticulously, given us the knowledge, the process, for remaining in contact with Kṛṣṇa-sūrya, and not just for a short interlude, a rare day in Kali-yuga before the winter returns, but for eternity, by going back home, back to the eternal youth, Lord Kṛṣṇa, the transcendental Cupid, who is served by eternal springtime in His unending pastimes of bliss.

We pray that we, like the winter sunbather, shall feel great discomfort from the chill brought on by even a momentary obstruction of the sun, and thereby remain always diligent in our endeavor to keep Kṛṣṇa-sūrya always in our minds and hearts. We pray also that our hearts may become perfectly pure, so we may reflect that lifelight, which we have received from you, back into those cold, dark caverns where so many *conditioned jīvas* yet remain in their bleak nightmares, and where we also huddled, such a short, long time ago.

*Your eternal servants,*

Stambha dāsa / Giri-yādhava dāsa / Uddhava dāsa / Nārada Muni dāsa / Bhakta Dave / Bhakta Ralph / Rudrāṇī-devī dāsī

## Switzerland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.*

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

The last time I wanted to write a Vyāsa-pūjā offering to you, I was on *saṅkīrtana.* It was afternoon, and I sat in the *saṅkīrtana* van. When I was in the midst of thinking what to write in order to properly glorify you, a car parked next to our van. An elderly lady stepped out of the car and looked at me, while the windows of our van were open. I said to her, "Good afternoon. How are you?”

She answered, "Fine, and to which sect do you belong?”

I said to her, "Have you never received a book from us?" and I stretched out a *Science of Self-Realization* towards her.

"Oh, I already have about three of these books at home, but I have never understood them."

I told her, "Please try this one; it is easier to understand. These are all lectures from the author of the other books. It is a wonderful book." And in this way I explained to her the philosophy in a nutshell. She became very inspired, and at the end she gave me 100 Swiss francs. In addition I gave her a *Bhagavad-gitā.* This incident enlivened me very much, and I became very enthusiastic to write an offering.

Another time I met a man who was just leaving his car, along with his wife. When he saw your picture, he asked me, "Who is this man, and what is his name?”

I told him, "It is His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda."

"What do you have to do with him?" he replied. I said, "He is my teacher."

The man was very astonished. Then I asked him why he asked. He said, "I have seen this man in 1975 in San Francisco."

"Oh, yeah!" I said. "This was probably on the Ratha-yātrā chariot festival."

He said, "Yes. " By remembering you he forgot everything around him. After the talk, he remembered his wife, who in the meantime had gone on without him. He then slipped a donation into my hand and went on walking. Regarding this person, and I think thousands of others who once met, saw or heard you, we can see that your influence is very great. It is said, *sādhu-saṅga sādhu-saṅga sarve śāstre koy lava mātra sādhu- saṅga sarva-siddhi hoy.* When we have only one moment's association with a *sādhu* like you, all the perfection of life can be achieved, and such a moment can never be forgotten in the whole life. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you live eternally in the hearts and the minds of the fallen souls. When you were personally present in your *vapuḥ* form, you showed us how one can use his body, mind, intelligence and words in the service of Kṛṣṇa and how one should remember Kṛṣṇa in all situations of life. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you go anywhere to deliver the fallen souls, irrespective of whether these places be so-called favorable or unfavorable.

*nārāyaṇa-parāḥ sarve*  
*na kutaścana bibhyati*  
*svargāpavarga-narakeṣv*  
*api tulyārtha-darśinaḥ*  
*SB 6.17.28*

You are always connected with the ocean of mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. And as Lord Nityānanda built a channel which over-flowed the whole area of Bengal, similarly you have directed here a channel in the West so that everyone can dive in love with Kṛṣṇa.

Normally it is very difficult to glorify you, because most of the devotees here in Switzerland had your association only for a couple of minutes or hours. Nevertheless, by your causeless mercy you go on living in the form of your instructions, mostly in the form of your books. Your books are our life and soul. We are fools and blockheads. We are slow to comprehend, and even unteachable; nevertheless, you engage us in your service. By your mercy we have got now all your books in the German language, and we know that you are very enlivened when we are able to deliver a full set of these books to the conditioned souls, so that each respectable family has a whole set on their bookshelf. Now already many people have several books at home, sometimes five to ten or even more, but as you once said, "Don't think our work is done. Don't rest on your laurels. There is still a lot of work to perform." Now almost all of your new German books have been distributed, and we have to reprint new ones. All this happens only by your mercy.

By your mercy, even here in materialistic Switzerland, whose churches are the banks and whose only opulences are rocks, snow and rain, the ocean of love of Godhead is rising higher and higher. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we all desire more and more that we may become fixed in your devotional service and that we may dive more and more in the ocean of nectar of love and devotion to Kṛṣṇa.

*Your servants of the Swiss Yātrā.*

## Sydney

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your transcendental lotus feet. In Śrī Śrī Gurvaṣṭaka it is stated, “The spiritual master is always engaged in the temple worship of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. He also engages his disciples in such worship. They dress the Deities in beautiful clothes and ornaments, clean Their temple, and perform other similar worship of the Lord. I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master.”

In 1971 you blessed Sydney by bringing Śrī Śrī Rādha-Gopīnātha to this land of the fallen, “*mleccha-deśa*.” You engaged us in the temple worship of the Lord and prayed to Lord Gopīnātha to please give these Australian boys and girls the intelligence to worship Him. When later you returned here, you told us you could see by the beautiful clothing and appearance of the Deities that Lord Gopīnātha had fulfilled your prayer.

For some time Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha have resided at a nearby ISKCON farm, but now they have returned to Sydney. Once again you are engaging us in Their temple worship.

All the devotees worked especially hard to prepare the temple for Their Lordships' arrival, making extensive and expensive renovations. Now more than ever we have become especially careful to keep the temple spotlessly clean. The whole idea of coming to this temple has taken on a wonderful new meaning for us. It is the spiritual abode of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. Preparing for the arrival of Their Lordships has been miraculous, for it has been just like preparing for the arrival of Your Divine Grace years ago. We think it must be your special mercy upon us.

In Sydney there are always too few devotees to do all of the tasks that need to be done. Even though new devotees constantly join us, there are more projects-a new restaurant, vegetarian clubs on university campuses, free *prasādam* distribution to the city's poor and distribution of millions of your books. We always seem to be busier than seems reasonsable. Therefore we have to be cent percent engaged in devotional service in order to carry on. We think this, too, must be your special mercy upon us.

Now we are a family of your disciples and your granddisciples. Although one might expect to find differences between us, we are united in your service, ever enlivened by the spirit of *saṅkīrtana.* We are standing together as a united ISKCON, setting the foundation for a wonderful Kṛṣṇa conscious future. This is what you wanted. That we can do it can only be your mercy. Every morning the new *bhakta’s,* initiated devotees, *brāhmaṇas,* and all the older Godbrothers and Godsisters are there at *maṅgala-ārati* and *tulasī-ārati,* and they chant their rounds together. Without them all, the *saṅga* would not be complete. Together we hear and chant Hare Kṛṣṇa enthusiastically. This, too, is what you wanted, and this, too, must be your mercy.

We are fallen and unable to deserve the service of your lotus feet or the feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha. We are incompetent to guide the world to Kṛṣṇa consciousness, but by putting all of our effort into purchasing, printing and distributing your books, we know you are personally present to make everything go right. If you weren't with us, how could all of the wonderful progress we are seeing unfold be possible? Please never let us stray, and eternally engage us in practicing and preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness for your pleasure.

*Your servants,*  
*The devotees of ISKCON Sydney*

## Taiwan

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā*  
*dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ*  
*karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.”** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.22)**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble and fallen obeisances at your lotus feet. Again and again we place our heads in the cooling shade of your lotus feet.

As always, on this most auspicious occasion of your divine appearance, we are feeling helplessly inadequate when faced with the task of properly glorifying Your Divine Grace. Like a dwarf trying to clutch at the moon, the result is that we only fall on our faces in the mud. Actually, we would require hundreds and millions of mouths and tongues to properly describe your glories. So, to quote your description of your *Guru* Mahārāja, "What can we say, Śrīla Prabhupāda? You are a Vaikuṇṭha man."

Śrīla Prabhupāda, who knows the sufferings of the world before you came? Darkness and gloom pervaded the land, and *māyā* reigned supreme. Bleached bones of armies littered the fields of almost every country .The cries of helpless animals being slaughtered and of helpless women being exploited caused the hearts of all devotees to crack. Yellowing pages of history mock man's folly with epitaphs of innumerable empires rising and falling. Where was the hope of salvation near at hand? We were like frogs in the snake jaws of death. What hope for escape was there? You were that hope, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Just as the thunder and lightning flashes new hope across the darkened sky, you illuminated our hearts and minds for the first time in perhaps millions of births. Just as the sun-scorched earth with all her living entities sighs with relief with the coming of the mercy rains of summer, so the *suffering jīvas* of the world cry with relief upon the arrival of your mercy rains of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are like the most welcome rain. That mercy rain is torrential and unlimited in scale.

*saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka-*  
*trāṇāya kāruṇya-ghanāghanatvam*  
*prāptasya kalyāṇa-guṇārṇavasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

You have come to extinguish the blazing forest fire of material existence, and therefore the universe is saved! Simply we cannot see it yet with these tiny peacock eyes.

*cakhu-dān dilo jei, janme janme prabhu sei,*  
*divya jñān hṛde prokāśito*  
*prema-bhakti jāhā hoite, avidyā vināśa jāte,*  
*vede gāy jāhāra carito*

We can only hope to see by the mercy of the *guru.* And of course, your mercy is there; it is always there for the sincere soul, and it is all-powerful!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you taught us that this is a world of dreams, shadows of reality, phantasmagoria. The opulences of man, like health, wealth, beautiful body, high position, palatial buildings, automobiles, women, children, etc., are destined to vanish like the will-o'-the-wisp, or like so much sea foam dashed on the rocks. Just as seasonal flowers appear so beautiful and fragrant one day but are gone the next, so in this world either the flowers wither or the gardener himself must leave. We are *not* these bodies, flesh and blood vehicles of pain and foolishness! We are spirit-eternal parts and parcels, servants of Śrī Śrī Rādhikā- Mādhava.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are helpless and hopeless without your mercy. Even with your blessings we are weaklings, and therefore we require special mercy. Please don't let us be cheated by *māyā* again. Please don't let us be distracted by the cheap, glittering allurements offered us in the form of society, friendship and love. They are like nonsensical toys meant to distract babies from their miseries, when actually the child cries out of hunger. We are hungering for service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa. And we are hankering for service to your lotus feet. We know that it is impossible even after millions of years to ever repay our debt to you, but somehow we must try. And that is our greatest fortune, our obligation to you. And to prove our love and gratitude to you, all your genuine sons and daughters must work in a spirit of cooperation and service to our *gurubhāis.* That means little brothers assisting bigger brothers. What is the difficulty? Of course, there is no *real* difficulty, only our causeless unsubmissive attitude and childishness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once told us that it was your desire that you could always be preaching and fighting for Kṛṣṇa, just like Arjuna on the battlefield of Kurukṣetra. And actually it's a fact. You never retired, but went on preaching full time and nonstop, fighting for Kṛṣṇa. And even your divine disappearance from this mortal world was all-glorious, just like that of grandfather Bhīṣma, a strong warrior preaching with the last breath. You said at that time, "These people have come to see if this old man has died yet, but you tell them that I am still here beating on this drum, the *bṛhad-mṛdaṅga."* And your *bṛhad-mṛdaṅga* is still beating, louder than ever, all over the world. Your potencies are ever expanding.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is our fervent prayer that we can somehow enter into that warrior spirit of preaching, fighting for Śrī Kṛṣṇa! On your order, we want to march around the world beating on your *bṛhad- mṛdaṅga,* like war drums sounding and resounding your victory. We will smash the demons and enemies of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. We will slash at their demoniac mentality with the razor-sharp sword of unconditional truth, Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Here in your new Taiwan Yātrā, the Chinese people have a very nice saying: "Death can be as light as a feather, or as heavy as a mountain." To die like a dog in fear and ignorance is certainly as heavy as a mountain. But to give up this body while doing something substantial in life, in service to *Guru* and Gaurāṅga, and with full knowledge of our true spiritual nature, can indeed be as light as a feather.

O unlimitedly merciful father, O light of the world, please accept some little service from us, although we are more of a pest than a servant. You may beat us if you like, but please, please don't kick us away.

Begging to become,  
*Your eternalservants.at ISKCON Taiwan,*  
Sevānanda dāsa / Pitā dāsa / Yaśomati-suta dāsa / Śravaṇīyā-devī dāsī

## Tehran

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We are worthless and totally unqualified. We do not know what the future will bring. We only hope that somehow or other we will be able to please you by our humble efforts to serve your movement here in Tehran. We do not get the association we so badly need here. Our communication with ISKCON and our seniors is limited. Our environment is hostile and unpredictable. We beg you, Prabhupāda, to help us make advancement in hearing and chanting of the holy name, so that we can realize your transcendental presence at every moment.

*Your servants at Karaj Farm, Tehran Yātrā*

## Trinidad

**Homage to Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Let me offer my respectful obeisances unto my beloved spiritual master, who has become the source of my existence and the very life of ISKCON and the only hope and savior for this whole world, especially the eternal lifeline for his devotees and myself.

O spiritual master, I wish that I may make the teachings from your lotus mouth one with my heart and not desire anything else. The attachment to your instructions, O servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvati, is the best means for advancement. By your unlimited mercy all our desires for spiritual perfection can be achieved.

O my spiritual master, ocean of mercy, the friend of every devotee, the eternal savior of this world, please bless me so that I may not have any other desire but to fulfill your desires. I understand that this is taking the shade of your lotus feet. May your glories now be proclaimed all over the three worlds, O servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī.

*Your children and grandchildren of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopinātha Mandira, Trinidad,*

## Vancouver

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

As your glories are recounted in the *Śrīla Prabhupāda-līlamṛta,* each delicious nectarean pastime enters into the hearts of the devotees and increases their hankering to be with you. Every devotee wants to break his head on the rocks lamenting, "Oh, why did I not get more association with Śrīla Prabhupāda and serve him with more sincerity?" When you were present with us on the earthly plane we could only appreciate a tiny fragment of your glorious character and pastimes. It is almost impossible for the impure to appreciate the pure or for the crude and dull to perceive the beautiful and perfect; nevertheless, the little we could perceive shattered our impersonal gloom and awoke the revolution of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in our hearts.

The early devotees had little understanding of the philosophy, but they knew *kīrtana, prasādam,* and especially they were convinced by you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You so perfectly reflect the qualities of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa that you brought an instant transfusion of faith to the deserted hearts of your followers. After touching the spiritual touchstone their faith and ecstasy were easily transmitted to others, and thus the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement was born.

Your unique and wonderful position in the line of great Vaiṣṇavas is truly special. Lord Nityānanda is famous for His great mercy in delivering the rascals Jagāi and Mādhāi. But Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Lord Nityānanda have sent you, and you have delivered thousands and thousands of loitering rascals into pure devotees of the Lord. The six Gosvāmīs rediscovered the pastime places of the Lord and built great temples in Vṛndāvana; but you are building a great temple and city in Māyāpur that will bring the whole world to the lotus feet of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

You are the captain of the ship (after building it and recruiting the crew), and you have set sail across the ocean of material life to bring a multitude to the side of the spiritual world. Although you have apparently left the actual seat of the captain's chair, you have left exact charts, if a trained crew, and you have asked Lord Kṛṣṇa to keep the favorable breezes blowing steadily towards the safe side.

We hope that you will be pleased to know that New Gokula is experiencing a great expansion of preaching facility. This year Śrī Śrī Rādhā- Madanamohana's temple is being finished at last. It is the second temple in North America to display Vedic domes, *cakras,* etc., and already the media and public are showing appreciation and interest. We have just completed the deal on a 1,650-acre valley farm which we plan to use as a *bhakta* retreat and as a home for our *expanding gurukula.* Moreover, we are projected to double our book distribution this year, and so far we are on schedule.

Although this expansion, which is only due to your causeless mercy, is increasing the weight of responsibility for all the devotees, we pray that this burden of love will crush all material desires and allow us to serve your mission eternally.

*Your servants in New Gokula,*
